Second, on creation and improvement, because each skill is schema-validated, functionally testable, and addressable node-by-node, failures can be diagnosed and repaired precisely. Two authored-skill failures were traced to the script level. After adjusting the AIP spec and recompiling, both recovered with zero regressions (one task going from 0/5 to 5/5), turning skill improvement into a measurable tuning loop rather than a prose rewrite. That same graph structure supports corpus-level governance and skill introspection, and provides a natural action space for reinforcement learning over skills.

</details>


### [115] [BiasGRPO: Stabilizing Bias Mitigation in High-Variance Reward Landscapes via Group-Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04807)
*Saket Reddy,Ke Yang,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BiasGRPO框架，利用组相对策略优化（GRPO）通过组归一化奖励来稳定LLM去偏对齐，在多个基准上优于DPO和PPO，并发布了高效、可集成的偏奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的社会偏见缓解面临独特挑战：偏见缺乏单一真实标准，导致高方差、主观的奖励景观。现有偏好微调方法存在关键权衡——DPO缺乏在线探索，PPO因批评者估计不可靠导致训练不稳定。

Method: 提出BiasGRPO框架，使用组相对策略优化（GRPO），用组相对基线替代价值函数，通过对一组采样完成进行奖励归一化来稳定对齐，同时保留在线训练的探索收益。此外，合成扩展了多领域数据集，并创建了高效、避免知识退化的偏奖励模型。

Result: BiasGRPO在多个基准上优于DPO和PPO，表明其有效性；定制的偏奖励模型计算高效且避免了知识退化。

Conclusion: BiasGRPO通过组归一化奖励有效稳定了LLM去偏对齐训练，其奖励模型可无缝集成到多目标RLHF流水线中，为偏见缓解提供了实用资源。

Abstract: Mitigating social bias in Large Language Models (LLMs) presents a distinct alignment challenge: unlike verifiable tasks, bias lacks a single ground truth, creating a high-variance, subjective reward landscape. Previous preference-based fine-tuning methods have major trade-offs: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is limited by the lack of exploration inherent in offline training, while Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) can lead to training instability due to potentially unreliable critic estimates. In this paper, we propose BiasGRPO, a framework using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to stabilize alignment by normalizing rewards across a group of sampled completions. By substituting the value function with a group-relative baseline, our approach reduces instability while maintaining the exploration benefits of online training. We find that BiasGRPO outperforms DPO and PPO across multiple benchmarks, indicating its effectiveness. To adapt GRPO, we synthetically extend a dataset spanning multiple domains and contexts. We also create and release a custom bias reward model that effectively guides generation while being highly compute-efficient and avoiding knowledge degradation, providing a valuable resource that can be seamlessly integrated into multi-objective RLHF pipelines.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Objective Equivalence: Constraint Injection for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling on Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04816)
*Xizi Luo,Changhong He,Dongdong Geng,Chenggong Shi,Yu Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出约束注入（constraint injection）方法，通过可行探测和单约束违反探测来暴露LLM生成优化代码中的虚假约束和约束遗漏，结合差分测试形成双重验证器；基于此构建VRPCoder模型，在21种VRP变体上达到93% Pass@1，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于目标等价信号（差分测试、答案一致性）的数据过滤方法无法检测非绑定约束的虚假添加或遗漏，对于约束密集的运筹优化问题而言不够可靠。

Method: 约束注入方法，包含可行探测（expose spurious over-constraint）和单约束违反探测（reveal silent constraint omission），与差分测试结合构成双重验证器；该验证器用作数据合成时的拒绝采样过滤和GRPO训练中的逐样本奖励。

Result: VRPCoder-GRPO在4个VRP基准上平均Pass@1达93%，在3个基准上超越Gemini-3.1-Pro Preview，平均超Claude-Sonnet-4.5 28个点，超先前OR-LLM 78个点。

Conclusion: 约束注入能有效检测优化代码中的约束级错误，是提升LLM生成约束密集优化代码正确性的有效方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly translate natural-language optimization problems into executable solver code. Yet for constraint-dense operations research (OR) problems, existing data-filtering and training pipelines largely rely on objective-equivalence signals such as differential testing and answer agreement, which a program can pass while adding spurious constraints or silently omitting required ones, whenever those constraints are non-binding on the tested instance. We propose constraint injection, which uses feasible probes to expose spurious over-constraint and one-constraint-violating probes to reveal silent constraint omission. Combined with differential testing, it forms a dual verifier. We instantiate and evaluate it on vehicle routing problems (VRPs), a representative constraint-dense combinatorial optimization testbed with coupled operational constraints. We develop VRPCoder, an 8B end-to-end model that translates natural-language VRP scenarios into Gurobi scripts, together with an expert-verified VRP benchmark suite covering 21 variants. The verifier is reused as a rejection-sampling filter during data synthesis and as a per-rollout reward in group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across four VRP benchmarks, VRPCoder-GRPO reaches 93\% average Pass@1, outperforms Gemini-3.1-Pro Preview on three benchmarks, exceeds Claude-Sonnet-4.5 by 28 average points, and surpasses prior OR-LLMs by 78 average points.

</details>


### [117] [R-APS: Compositional Reasoning and In-Context Meta-Learning for Constrained Design via Reflective Adversarial Pareto Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04823)
*João Pedro Gandarela,Thiago Rios,Stefan Menzel,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出R-APS方法，通过推理模式分解、多时间尺度交互和结构化协议设计，在无需微调的情况下解决LLM在智能体任务中的三个耦合结构失效问题（错误传播、最坏情况扰动、知识积累），在平面机构综合任务上显著优于基线方法，并发现小模型在结构化协议中可接近大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在开放式任务中表现流畅，但在需要规划、使用工具和长期行动的智能体场景中不可靠。作者将其归因于三个耦合的结构性失效：错误传播无法定位、最坏情况扰动未评估、累积知识从未失效。这些失效的根源在于溯因、反事实、元归纳、修正和归纳五种推理模式共享同一上下文时产生的不兼容冲突。

Method: R-APS通过推理模式分解为每种推理模式分配独立上下文，并在三个时间尺度上编排交互：1）分阶段组合推理+类型化验证批评器（定位失败）；2）敏感性引导的反事实压力测试作为一阶帕累托目标（鲁棒性）；3）元归纳规则提取+显式失效机制（持久记忆）。R-APS无需微调，仅通过结构化协议设计操作冻结的LLM。

Result: 在32个目标轨迹上的平面机构综合任务中，R-APS相比均匀扰动基线获得3.5倍更紧的鲁棒性证书，迭代到首次接纳速度快46%，Chamfer距离相比Enum+GA降低2.1倍，同时联合控制杆数和工作最坏情况鲁棒性。4B推理专用小模型在协议内与70B通用大模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 结构化协议设计可以部分抵消模型规模的影响，R-APS为LLM在需要可靠性的智能体任务中提供了一种无需微调的有效范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are fluent on open-ended tasks, yet in agentic settings, where a system must plan, use tools, and act over extended horizons, fluency does not ensure reliable delivery. We trace this gap to three coupled structural failures: errors propagate without localization, worst-case perturbations go unevaluated, and accumulated knowledge is never invalidated. We argue these share a root cause: abductive, counterfactual, meta-inductive, corrective, and inductive reasoning pull a shared context in incompatible directions. We introduce Reflective Adversarial Pareto Search (R-APS), to our knowledge the first method addressing all three failures jointly via reasoning-mode decomposition, allocating each reasoning mode its own context and orchestrating interaction across three timescales: staged compositional reasoning with a typed validation critic (failure localization), sensitivity-guided counterfactual stress-testing as a first-class Pareto objective (robustness), and meta-inductive rule extraction with explicit invalidation (persistent memory). R-APS requires no fine-tuning and operates on a frozen LLM purely via structured protocol design. We evaluate on planar mechanism synthesis (robotics, prosthetics, mechanical design), with every candidate checked by a kinematic solver. On 32 target trajectories, R-APS delivers robustness certificates 3.5x tighter than uniform-perturbation baselines, 46% faster iterations-to-first-admission, and 2.1x Chamfer-distance reduction over Enum+GA while jointly controlling bar-count and worst-case robustness. Small 4B reasoning-specialized models prove competitive with general-purpose 70B backbones inside the protocol, suggesting structured protocols can partially offset model scale.

</details>


### [118] [AICompanionBench: Benchmarking LLMs-as-Judges for AI Companion Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04867)
*Yanjing Ren,Reza Ebrahimi,TengTeng Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AICompanionBench，首个公开的、细粒度标注安全风险类别的人机伴侣对话基准数据集（包含2,123条真实Replika对话，涵盖9类安全风险），并评估了20种大语言模型在该基准上的不安全交互检测能力。结果显示，现有模型虽能有效检测显式有害内容，但在识别操纵等隐式不安全交互及避免误报方面仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Replika、Character.AI等AI伴侣平台快速增长，人机交互中的安全问题日益突出，但目前缺乏针对AI伴侣对话的细粒度安全风险基准数据集，难以系统评估和监测这类系统中的不安全交互。

Method: 从Reddit收集2,123条真实Replika对话，通过人机协作（human-AI collaboration）方式在9个安全风险类别（性行为、反社会行为、身体攻击、言语攻击、药物滥用、自残与自杀、控制、操纵、无危害）上进行细粒度标注；采用LLM-as-judge框架评估20种开源和闭源大语言模型的检测性能。

Result: 强模型整体准确率较高，但在操纵等细微类别以及将无害对话误判为有害（假阳性）方面仍表现不佳；不同模型间性能差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型能有效检测显式有害内容，但在识别隐式不安全交互方面能力有限；该基准数据集为AI伴侣安全研究提供了重要资源，并为使用LLM监测AI伴侣系统提供了见解。

Abstract: As AI companion platforms such as Replika and Character.AI rapidly grow, concerns about unsafe human-AI interactions have intensified. This study introduces AICompanionBench, to our knowledge the first publicly available benchmark dataset of human-AI companion conversations annotated with fine-grained safety risk categories. The dataset contains 2,123 real-world Replika conversations collected from Reddit and annotated through human-AI collaboration across nine categories: sexual behavior, antisocial behavior, physical aggression, verbal aggression, substance abuse, self-harm and suicide, control, manipulation, and no-harm. Using this benchmark, we evaluate 20 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source LLMs under an LLM-as-judge framework for detecting unsafe interactions. Results show substantial variation in model performance, with stronger models achieving high overall accuracy but still struggling with nuanced categories such as manipulation, as well as benign conversations that are incorrectly identified as harmful. Our findings suggest that while current LLMs can effectively detect explicit harmful content, they remain limited in identifying implicit unsafe interactions. Overall, our work contributes a new benchmark dataset for AI companionship safety research and offers insights into monitoring AI companion systems using LLMs. The dataset is publicly available at: https://github.com/anonymousresearcher2026/AICompanionBench/blob/main/AICompanionBench.xlsx

</details>


### [119] [What Type of Inference is Active Inference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04935)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Mykola Lukashchuk,Thijs van de Laar,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明预期自由能（EFE）最小化可重写为带显式熵校正项的自由能变分，揭示EFE规划需要结合认知校正与规划校正，并在网格世界实验中验证了不同校正项的适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将主动推理中的EFE最小化统一为变分自由能（VFE）框架，澄清EFE与VFE之间的理论关系，并明确实现完整EFE规划所需的具体校正项。

Method: 理论推导：将增强生成模型的VFE重写为预测模型VFE加显式熵校正项；引入规划校正（将边际推理转化为策略优化）；提出基于熵校正的消息传递方案。实验：在三个网格世界环境中比较不同校正项的效果。

Result: 规划校正能在观测具有决定性时提升效果；观测侧认知校正则在观测仅具暗示性时最为重要。

Conclusion: 完整EFE规划需要同时包含规划校正和认知校正，不同校正项在不同观测条件下发挥不同作用，所提出的熵校正框架为EFE规划提供了清晰的变分表征和可实现的消息传递方案。

Abstract: Active inference casts decision-making as inference, with the Expected Free Energy (EFE) unifying goal-directed and information-seeking behavior. Recent work showed that EFE minimization can be written as Variational Free Energy (VFE) minimization on a generative model augmented with epistemic priors. We prove that the VFE of the augmented model can be rewritten as the VFE of the predictive model plus explicit entropy-correction terms, making the EFE contribution transparent. We then show that proper EFE-based planning requires combining these epistemic corrections with a planning correction that turns marginal inference into policy optimization, yielding a full variational characterization of EFE-based planning. This clarifies which corrections are needed for cross-entropy planning and for full EFE-based planning. The same entropy-corrected formulation leads to a detailed message-passing scheme for EFE-based planning together with simpler ablations. Experiments on three grid-world environments show that the planning correction already helps when observations are decisive, whereas the additional observation-side epistemic corrections matter most when observations are merely suggestive.

</details>


### [120] [Strabo: Declarative Specification and Implementation of Agentic Interaction Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05043)
*Samuel H. Christie,Amit K. Chopra,Munindar P. Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了Strabo系统，通过将Google的UCP电商协议建模为声明式Langshaw协议，并用Peach编程模型实现智能体，证明了声明式交互协议在Agentic AI中的实用价值，且能与Google原生的UCP智能体互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来声明式多智能体系统取得了重大进展，但尚未与Google等行业巨头在Agentic AI方面的工业实践有效对接。作者希望弥合这一差距，证明声明式协议方法能直接应用于现实工业标准。

Method: 选取UCP协议中与结账相关的部分，将其建模为声明式Langshaw协议，并使用Peach编程模型实现相应的智能体。然后通过互操作性测试，验证Peach智能体与Google实现的UCP智能体能否协同工作。

Result: 成功将UCP结账流程建模为声明式协议并实现智能体，同时Peach智能体能够与Google原生的UCP智能体互操作，证明了声明式方法保真于UCP标准。

Conclusion: 声明式交互协议（如Langshaw）和编程模型（如Peach）可以增量地引入现有的工业Agentic AI系统，无需全面替换现有基础设施，为声明式多智能体系统（EMAS）思想在实践中的应用提供了一条可行的渐进式路径。

Abstract: The last few years have witnessed major advances in the modeling and implementation of multiagent systems based on declarative interaction protocols. Our contribution, Strabo, establishes the relevance of these advances to ongoing industry efforts in Agentic AI. Specifically, we consider UCP, the Universal Commerce Protocol, a recent Google-led effort to standardize e-commerce interactions for AI agents. Our exercise is in two parts. One, we model the part of UCP dealing with checkouts as a declarative Langshaw protocol and implement agents using Peach, a programming model for Langshaw. This part of the exercise brings out the advantages of formal, declarative specifications. Two, we show that Peach agents can interoperate with UCP agents implemented by Google, thereby establishing the fidelity of our approach with respect to UCP. Such interoperation enables the incremental introduction of declarative protocols and agents into a conventional setting, indicating a pathway by which EMAS ideas could influence practice without demanding a wholesale update.

</details>


### [121] [AutoLab: Can Frontier Models Solve Long-Horizon Auto Research and Engineering Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05080)
*Zhangchen Xu,Junda Chen,Yue Huang,Dongfu Jiang,Jiefeng Chen,Hang Hua,Zijian Wu,Zheyuan Liu,Zexue He,Lichi Li,Shizhe Diao,Jiaxin Pei,Jinsung Yoon,Hao Zhang,Mengdi Wang,Radha Poovendran,Misha Sra,Alex Pentland,Zichen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoLab是一个包含36个跨领域任务的基准测试，用于评估模型在长时间跨度下的闭环优化能力。实验表明，成功的关键不在于初始方案质量，而在于持续迭代和利用实证反馈的能力。Claude-opus-4.6表现优异，但多数前沿模型过早终止或进展有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估单轮响应或短时间跨度代理轨迹，无法捕捉长时间跨度下持续迭代改进的挑战，因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建包含36个专家策划任务的基准，涵盖系统优化、谜题挑战、模型开发和CUDA内核优化四个领域。每个任务从正确但次优的基线开始，要求代理在严格的时间预算内改进。评估了17个最先进模型。

Result: 成功的主要预测因素不是初始方案质量，而是持续进行基准测试、编辑和整合实证反馈的持久性。Claude-opus-4.6展现出强大的长时域优化能力，但多数前沿模型过早终止或在预算内进展甚微。

Conclusion: 时间感知和持续迭代在自主代理中至关重要。开源了完整基准、评估工具和任务工件，以加速长时域代理的研究。

Abstract: Scientific and engineering progress is fundamentally a long-horizon iterative process: proposing changes, running experiments, measuring outcomes, and continuously refining artifacts. Yet existing benchmarks for frontier models primarily evaluate either single-turn responses or short-horizon agent trajectories, failing to capture the challenges of sustained iterative improvement over extended time horizons. To address this gap, we introduce AutoLab, a new benchmark for ultra long-horizon closed-loop optimization. AutoLab consists of 36 realistic, expert-curated tasks spanning four diverse domains: system optimization, puzzle & challenge, model development, and CUDA kernel optimization. Each task begins with a correct but deliberately suboptimal baseline and challenges agents to improve it within a strict wall-clock budget. Evaluating 17 state-of-the-art models reveals the dominant predictor of success is not the quality of an agent's initial attempt, but its persistence in repeatedly benchmarking, editing, and incorporating empirical feedback. While claude-opus-4.6 exhibits strong long-horizon optimization capabilities, most frontier models, including several proprietary ones, either terminate prematurely or exhaust their budgets with minimal progress. These results underscore the importance of time awareness and persistent iteration in autonomous agents. We open-source the full benchmark, evaluation harness, and task artifacts, to accelerate research toward truly capable long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [122] [Knowledge Index of Noah's Ark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05104)
*Sheng Jin,Minghao Liu,Yunze Xiao,Zeqi Zhou,Heli Qi,Yifan Yao,Meishu Song,Kaijing Ma,Xuan Zhang,Sicong Jiang,Yizhe Li,Ningshan Ma,Jie Wei,Ziniu Li,Minglai Yang,Bangya Liu,Yiming Liang,Xiao Fang,Qingcheng Zeng,Jiarui Liu,Rui Yang,Shen Yan,Wenhao Huang,Jiaheng Liu,Zihan Wang,Weihao Xuan,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KINA是一个包含261个细粒度学科、899个项目的LLM知识基准，通过覆盖率目标的形式化表示和锦标赛激励支付机制，解决了现有基准的学科代表性不足、标注激励扭曲和排名不稳定三个问题。在42个模型评估中，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview仅达53.17%，表明仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM知识基准存在三个问题：1) 基于规模扩展的设计未操作化学科代表性；2) 固定报酬的标注方式导致"懒惰共识"；3) 在有限测试预算下排名不稳定且缺乏审计。因此需要构建一个更具代表性、激励相容且排名稳定的知识基准。

Method: 1) 将学科代表性形式化为覆盖目标函数，通过专家引出的锚点，利用代理目标实现(1-1/e)的贪心近似保证（命题1）；2) 设计"奖金阶梯"锦标赛机制，证明其在已发布质量上弱一阶随机占优固定报酬，并给出了激励相容阈值B > ΔC/Δp_min（定理1）；3) 采用bootstrap重抽样计算排名稳定性统计量。

Result: 评估13个实验室的42个模型，Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview以53.17%位居第一，Claude-Opus-4.6 (49.92%)和GPT-5.4 (48.55%)紧随其后。排名呈分层结构：前沿层(>48%)、强模型层(38-45%)和低性能层(略高于10%随机基线)。工具增强平均提升最多5.17分，但模型间差异显著。

Conclusion: KINA基准揭示了顶级模型与理论饱和之间仍有近50个百分点的巨大差距，现有LLM在细粒度学科知识上远未饱和；排名分层结构表明模型能力并非连续谱系；bootstrap统计量提醒不应过度解读相邻排名的差异。

Abstract: Knowledge benchmarks for LLMs face three issues: scaling-driven designs that do not operationalize disciplinary representativeness; flat-payment annotation that permits lazy consensus; and unaudited ranking instability under bounded test budgets. We introduce KINA, an 899-item benchmark across 261 fine-grained disciplines, with two formal results. First, we cast representativeness as a coverage-style objective over expert-elicited anchors and operationalize disciplinary representativeness through a proxy, yielding a (1-1/e) greedy approximation (Proposition 1); the guarantee applies to the proxy, not to population representativeness. Second, we prove a bonus-on-bar tournament weakly FOSD-dominates flat payment in released-review quality, with incentive-compatibility threshold B > Delta C / Delta p_min (Theorem 1). Evaluating 42 models from 13 labs, the top model, Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview, reaches 53.17%, followed by Claude-Opus-4.6 at 49.92% and GPT-5.4 at 48.55%, leaving substantial headroom below saturation. The full leaderboard shows a tiered structure rather than a smooth total order: a small frontier tier lies above 48%, a dense strong-model tier spans roughly 38-45%, and low-performing models remain only modestly above the 10% chance baseline. Tool augmentation adds up to 5.17 points across the five tool-use evaluations, with gains varying substantially across models. We report bootstrap ranking-stability statistics to make bounded-budget variance explicit and to discourage over-interpretation of adjacent ranks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Early Detection of Alzheimer's Disease Using Explainable Machine Learning on Clinical Biomarkers: A Multi-Class Classification Study Using the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03995)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于XGBoost和8项常规临床评估指标（MMSE、CDR Global、CDR-SB、MoCA、FAQ、年龄、性别、教育程度），使用SMOTE处理类别不平衡，结合SHAP可解释性分析，在ADNI数据集上实现了近乎完美的三分类（NC/MCI/AD）阿尔茨海默病检测，宏AUC达0.982，准确率0.943。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过5500万AD患者，目前缺乏能够从常规临床评估中准确、可解释地检测正常认知、轻度认知障碍和阿尔茨海默病的方法，这是一个关键的未满足临床需求。

Method: 使用XGBoost分类器进行三分类检测，输入特征为ADNI数据集中的8项临床指标；采用Optuna（50次试验）优化超参数，SMOTE处理类别不平衡；通过5折交叉验证和独立测试集评估性能，使用SHAP值提供特征级可解释性。

Result: 1,641名基线受试者（608 NC, 767 MCI, 266 AD）。5折交叉验证：宏AUC 0.983（SD 0.007），准确率0.944（SD 0.006），宏F1 0.929（SD 0.008）。测试集（n=247）：宏AUC 0.982（95% CI: 0.965–0.995），准确率0.943，平衡准确率0.932，宏F1 0.927，Cohen's kappa 0.909。SHAP分析显示CDR Global是NC和MCI的主要预测因子，CDR-SB和MMSE共同驱动AD分类。

Conclusion: 基于常规临床评估的可解释机器学习模型实现了近乎完美的三分类阿尔茨海默病检测。SHAP分析揭示了临床合理且类别特定的特征重要性模式，支持临床有效性。未来将引入语音生物标志物进行多模态检测。

Abstract: Background: Alzheimer's disease (AD) affects over 55 million people worldwide. Accurate, interpretable detection of normal cognition (NC), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and AD from routine clinical assessments remains a critical unmet need. Methods: An XGBoost classifier was developed for three-class detection using eight clinical features from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI): MMSE, CDR Global, CDR Sum of Boxes (CDR-SB), MoCA, FAQ, age, sex, and education. Hyperparameters were optimised using Optuna (50 trials); class imbalance was addressed with SMOTE. Performance was evaluated by macro AUC-ROC with 1,000-iteration bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, macro F1, balanced accuracy, and Cohen's kappa. SHAP values provided feature-level explainability. Results: The dataset comprised 1,641 baseline subjects (608 NC, 767 MCI, 266 AD). On five-fold cross-validation, mean macro AUC was 0.983 (SD 0.007), accuracy 0.944 (SD 0.006), and macro F1 0.929 (SD 0.008). On the held-out test set (n = 247), macro AUC was 0.982 (95% CI: 0.965--0.995), accuracy 0.943, balanced accuracy 0.932, macro F1 0.927, and Cohen's kappa 0.909. SHAP analysis identified CDR Global as the dominant predictor for NC and MCI, while CDR-SB and MMSE together drove AD classification. Conclusion: An explainable machine learning model trained on routine clinical assessments achieves near-perfect three-class Alzheimer's detection. SHAP analysis reveals clinically plausible, class-specific feature importance patterns supporting clinical validity. Future work will extend this framework with speech biomarkers for multimodal detection.

</details>


### [124] [Novel Aspects of IEEE SA P3109 Arithmetic Formats for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04028)
*Andrew Fitzgibbon,Christoph M. Wintersteiger,Jeffrey Sarnoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IEEE P3109标准草案定义了参数化的二进制浮点格式族及其运算，专为机器学习优化。格式通过宽度、精度、符号性和无穷存在性参数化，运算基于闭扩展实数集定义，支持多种舍入和饱和模式（包括随机舍入），无异常运算，通过返回值（如NaN）传达异常情况。块级运算统一实现，系统供应商可通过κ近似描述近似实现。标准函数定义和性质已通过形式化规范机械验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器学习应用提供高效、一致的短位宽浮点表示，解决传统浮点标准在低精度场景下的局限性，同时保证形式化验证的正确性。

Method: 采用参数化格式族设计（宽度、精度、符号性、无穷性）；基于闭扩展实数集解码浮点值；定义无异常运算（通过NaN传递异常）；引入κ近似作为尺度不变的近似度量；使用形式化规范进行机械验证。

Result: 定义了IEEE P3109标准草案，包含参数化浮点格式、多种舍入模式（含随机舍入）、无异常运算机制、块级统一运算、κ近似度量，以及经形式化验证的标准函数定义。

Conclusion: 该标准为机器学习场景提供了灵活、高效且形式化验证的低精度浮点算术标准，推动了AI硬件和软件的互操作性与可复现性。

Abstract: The IEEE P3109 draft standard defines a parameterized family of binary floating-point formats and associated operations, with a focus on facilitating machine learning. These formats allow efficient and consistent representation of values in a small number of bits. The defined formats are parameterized over width and precision in bits, signedness, and the presence of infinities. Operations are defined by decoding floating-point values to the set of closed extended reals: the reals augmented with positive and negative infinity and NaN (Not a Number). Explicit treatment of NaN and infinite operands ensures that only real arithmetic is invoked in operation definitions. Extensive rounding and saturation modes are defined; stochastic rounding is included. Operations are exception-free, accelerating throughput, with exceptional situations communicated through return values, e.g., NaN. Operations on blocks of values sharing a common scale factor are defined in terms of the underlying operations in a uniform manner. System vendors may describe approximate implementations via a novel scale-invariant measure, akin to units in the last place, called kappa-approximation. Standard function definitions and various other properties are mechanically verified and generated using formal specifications.

</details>


### [125] [Position: Deployed Reinforcement Learning should be Continual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04029)
*Parnian Behdin,Kevin Roice,Golnaz Mesbahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前强化学习系统采用“训练-修复”范式，部署后无法持续学习，本质上是一个持续强化学习问题。作者识别了部署后四种非平稳性来源，分析了实际成功案例，并呼吁社区转向永不停止学习的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL系统采用train-then-fix范式，部署后无法持续适应环境变化，导致性能退化时必须重新训练，缺乏终身学习能力。

Method: 通过理论分析识别部署后四种非平稳性来源（环境变化、任务漂移、分布偏移等），并结合实际成功案例论证持续RL的必要性和可行性。

Result: 论证了部署后持续学习的根本必要性，指出最优代理应永不停止适应，并总结了实际应用中持续RL的成功经验。

Conclusion: 呼吁RL社区从train-then-fix范式转向持续学习范式，并给出了相应的优势评估与实现路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has received increasing attention and adoption in real-world use cases. Most of these systems follow a train-then-fix paradigm, where trained agents do not learn while interacting with the world until performance degrades and retraining becomes necessary. In this position paper, we argue that deploying an agent that is incapable of optimality, but receives an evaluative reward signal, is inherently a continual RL problem. We identify four sources of non-stationarity after deployment that necessitate never-ending learning, and highlight why the best deployed agents never stop adapting. We analyze successful examples of continual RL in the real world, and present the community with the advantages and measures to move away from the current train-then-fix paradigm.

</details>


### [126] [Pseudospectral Bounds for Transient Amplification in Coupled Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04031)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合梯度下降法的块三角雅可比矩阵的伪谱理论，证明Kreiss常数的上界并建立极小极大下界，揭示非渐近、实例相关的高维学习动力学标度律，实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合梯度下降法在双层优化、两时间尺度随机逼近和对抗训练中广泛存在，但块三角雅可比矩阵的非正态性会导致收敛前的瞬态放大任意大，而谱半径分析无法捕捉这一现象，因此需要建立更精细的理论来刻画动力学行为。

Method: 发展块三角雅可比矩阵的尖锐伪谱理论，证明对称对角块情况下Kreiss常数的上界并建立匹配的极小极大下界；刻画耦合阈值对谱稳定性的临界影响；通过Neumann级数扰动框架将分析推广到近自指系统；导出有限步迭代复杂度界。

Result: 证明了Kreiss常数满足K(J) ≤ 2/(1-γ) + ‖C‖/(4(1-γ))（γ<1为对角块谱半径上界），建立了匹配的极小极大下界；获得O(K(J)^2 log(1/δ))的随机耦合下降有限步迭代复杂度界；在LQ问题、IQC比较和神经网络训练中验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一类被谱半径分析所掩盖的非渐近、实例依赖的高维学习动力学标度律，为理解耦合梯度下降的瞬态行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Coupled gradient descent--where the update of one parameter block depends on another--underlies bilevel optimization, two-time-scale stochastic approximation, and adversarial training. When the coupled Jacobian is block-triangular, asymptotic stability is governed by the spectral radii of the diagonal blocks, yet transient amplification before convergence can be arbitrarily large due to non-normality. We develop a sharp pseudospectral theory for such block-triangular Jacobians, proving that the Kreiss constant satisfies $K(J) \leq 2/(1-γ) + \|C\|/(4(1-γ))$ when the diagonal blocks are symmetric with spectral radii at most $γ< 1$, and we establish matching minimax lower bounds. We characterize the critical coupling threshold for spectral instability and extend the analysis to nearly self-referential systems via a Neumann-series perturbation framework. As a consequence, we obtain a finite-horizon iteration-complexity bound of $O(K(J)^2 \log(1/δ))$ for stochastic coupled descent. Framed as scaling laws for non-stationary two-time-scale optimization, our results expose a non-asymptotic, instance-dependent regime of high-dimensional learning dynamics that is invisible to spectral-radius analysis. Experiments on linear-quadratic problems, IQC-based comparisons, and neural-network training confirm the theory.

</details>


### [127] [PE-MHL: Physics-Encoded Modular Hybrid Layers for Scalable Learning of Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04290)
*Ismail Hassaballa,Mircea Lazar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PE-MHL框架，通过增量添加物理编码子模型来逐步优化基础物理模型，保证训练误差单调非增且收敛，在非线性NARX基准和Quanser Aero 2平台上优于同等规模单体网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合模型在可扩展性、噪声鲁棒性和模型复杂度控制方面仍存在挑战，亟需一种既能保持物理一致性又能增量学习的高效框架。

Method: 提出物理编码模块化混合层（PE-MHL）框架，以最小二乘初始化每个新增子模型，确保训练误差随子模型数量单调非增且收敛，同时保持已学知识。

Result: 在非线性NARX基准和Quanser Aero 2平台上，PE-MHL在准确性和泛化性上均优于同等规模的单体网络，训练动态更稳定，且更好地保留底层数据结构。

Conclusion: PE-MHL通过增量模块化设计实现了物理一致性与数据驱动灵活性的有效平衡，为控制应用中的混合建模提供了理论保证与实证优势。

Abstract: Hybrid models that combine physics-based and data-driven components have shown strong potential for achieving accuracy and interpretability in control applications. While recent methods have made progress in incorporating physical consistency, challenges remain in scalability, robustness to noise, and control of model complexity. This paper proposes a Physics-Encoded Modular Hybrid Layer (PE-MHL) framework, in which a baseline physics-based model is incrementally refined through the addition of new sub-models, where each new component adds complexity while preserving what previous components have already learned. We establish a theoretical guarantee for this construction: with a least-squares initialization of each new sub-model, the training error is monotonically non-increasing in the number of sub-models and provably converges. Empirical evaluations on a nonlinear NARX benchmark and the Quanser Aero 2 platform demonstrate that PE-MHL outperforms equivalently sized monolithic networks in both accuracy and generalization, while also providing more stable training dynamics and better preservation of underlying data structures.

</details>


### [128] [Do Transformers Need Three Projections? Systematic Study of QKV Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04032)
*Ali Kayyam,Anusha Madan Gopal,M Anthony Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Transformer中QKV投影共享策略，发现Q-K=V（共享键值投影）在多数任务上性能与标准QKV相当甚至更优，在语言建模中可减少50% KV缓存且仅造成3.1%的困惑度损失，结合GQA/MQA可实现高达96.9%的缓存压缩，适合边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中QKV注意力机制是核心组件，但三个投影各自的贡献以及省略其中某些投影的影响尚未被充分理解，作者希望通过系统评估投影共享约束来探究这一问题。

Method: 系统评估三种投影共享约束：Q-K=V（共享键值）、Q=K-V（共享查询键）、Q=K=V（单一投影），并针对对称注意力图问题引入2D位置编码实现非对称注意力。实验涵盖合成任务、视觉任务（MNIST、CIFAR、TinyImageNet、异常检测）和语言建模（300M和1.2B参数模型在10B tokens上训练）。

Result: Q-K=V共享策略在多数任务上与标准QKV性能相当甚至更优；语言建模中Q-K=V实现50% KV缓存缩减，仅3.1%困惑度退化；Q-K=V与GQA-4结合达87.5%缓存缩减，与MQA结合达96.9%；Q=K-V和Q=K=V因破坏注意力方向性而性能下降。

Conclusion: Q-K=V共享有效是因为键和值可以占据相似的表示空间且注意力在低秩机制下运行；投影共享是注意力中权重绑定的未充分探索实例，可直接量化推理内存收益，特别适合边缘端部署。

Abstract: Transformers have become the standard solution for various AI tasks, with the query, key, and value (QKV) attention formulation playing a central role. However, the individual contribution of these three projections and the impact of omitting some remain poorly understood. We systematically evaluate three projection sharing constraints: a) Q-K=V (shared key-value), b) Q=K-V (shared query-key), and c) Q=K=V (single projection). The last two variants produce symmetric attention maps; to address this, we also explore asymmetric attention via 2D positional encodings. Through experiments spanning synthetic tasks, vision (MNIST, CIFAR, TinyImageNet, anomaly), and language modeling (300M and 1.2B parameter models on 10B tokens), we discovered that our transformers perform on par or occasionally better than the QKV transformer. In language modeling, Q-K=V projection sharing achieves 50% KV cache reduction with only 3.1% perplexity degradation. Crucially, projection sharing is complementary to head sharing (GQA/MQA): combining Q-K=V with GQA-4 yields 87.5% cache reduction, while Q-K=V + MQA achieves 96.9%, enabling practical on-device inference. We show that Q-K=V preserves quality because keys and values can occupy similar representational spaces and attention operates in a low-rank regime, whereas Q=K-V breaks attention directionality. Our results systematically characterize projection sharing as an underexplored instance of weight tying in attention, with direct, quantifiable inference memory benefits, particularly valuable for edge deployment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/anushamadan02/Do-Transformers-Need-3-Projections

</details>


### [129] [A Geometric Characterization of the Stationary Plateau for Two-Layer Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04327)
*Tian Ding,Dawei Li,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有平滑激活函数的两层神经网络损失景观中平稳高原的几何结构，重点关注“神经元分裂”现象，即复制隐藏神经元会在更宽网络中产生仿射平稳点集。通过引入“内Hessian”矩阵，对高原上所有平稳点进行了分类，确定了局部最小值或鞍点的条件。结果表明，分裂局部最小值可能产生局部极小与鞍点的混合或全鞍点高原，而分裂鞍点则始终产生鞍点高原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络损失景观中平稳高原的几何结构，特别是神经元分裂后更宽网络中平稳点的性质变化，以统一和扩展先前的景观分析，揭示模型扩展何时以及如何改变平稳点的性质。

Method: 提出“内Hessian”矩阵作为每神经元曲率对象，基于内Hessian的定性和分裂系数的选择，对平稳高原上的所有平稳点进行分类，分析其局部极小或鞍点性质。

Result: 内Hessian的定性和分裂系数共同决定高原局部几何结构；分裂局部最小值可产生混合类型或全鞍点高原，且在温和假设下可识别具体的“确保鞍点”区域；分裂鞍点始终产生鞍点高原。

Conclusion: 研究统一并扩展了先前损失景观分析，阐明了宽度扩展和重参数化对神经网络中平稳点性质影响的几何机制。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of stationary plateaus that arise in the loss landscape of two-layer neural networks with smooth activation functions. We focus on the phenomenon of "neuron splitting" where duplicating a hidden neuron yields an affine set of stationary points in a wider network. We provide a comprehensive classification of all stationary points on these plateaus, determining under what conditions they constitute local minima or saddle points. Our characterization hinges on a per-neuron curvature object we term the "inner Hessian" matrix. Our analysis reveals that the definiteness of the inner Hessian and the choice of splitting coefficients jointly dictate the local geometry of the plateau. We show that "splitting" a local minimum can yield either a mixture of local minima and saddles or an all-saddle plateau, with a concrete sure-saddle region identified under mild assumptions. In contrast, splitting a saddle point always produces a plateau of saddle points. Our results unify and extend prior landscape analyses, elucidating when and how model expansion preserves or alters the nature of stationary points. These findings offer new geometric insights into the effects of width expansion and reparameterization in neural networks.

</details>


### [130] [Inverse Critical Experiment Design via Gradient Optimization and a Multigroup Attention-Based Neural Network Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04033)
*Will Savage,Logan Burnett,Dean Price*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用深度神经网络代理建模和非参数梯度优化的方法，用于逆向设计临界实验，以最大化与目标核技术的中子相似性（相关系数$c_k$），并应用于TN-Americas TN-LC运输容器验证，最高达到$c_k=0.97757$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进核反应堆设计和燃料概念的验证需要与目标技术具有高中子相似性的临界实验，现有实验对HALEU燃料运输容器等技术的覆盖不足，而传统设计方法难以高效优化实验几何以最大化相似性指标$c_k$。

Method: 使用OpenMC计算基于网格的临界实验几何的灵敏度向量，训练U-Net卷积编码器-解码器结合多群注意力池化层的深度神经网络作为代理模型，利用其可微性通过非参数梯度优化直接改变每个网格位置的材料分配，最大化相关系数$c_k$。

Result: 对于三种感兴趣的配置，优化生成的实验几何分别达到$c_k$值为0.97757、0.81324和0.93276，其中两个配置超过0.9的相似性阈值。多群注意力池化层在性能和可解释性上均优于传统池化方法。

Conclusion: 深度学习代理建模与梯度优化的结合能够高效逆向设计临界实验几何，显著加速先进核技术的验证过程，展示了机器学习在核工程设计中应用的潜力。

Abstract: The validation of advanced nuclear reactor designs and fuel concepts requires critical experiments with high neutronic similarity to the target technology. Neutronic similarity is quantified by the correlation coefficient $c_k$, which captures the shared bias in $k_\text{eff}$ induced by uncertainties in nuclear data. Generally, a $c_k\geq0.9$ is needed for an experiment to be sufficiently similar to a target technology. This work presents a methodology for the inverse design of critical experiments. Deep neural network surrogate modeling and nonparametric gradient optimization are used to generate experiment geometries that maximize $c_k$.
  A deep neural network is trained on OpenMC-calculated sensitivity vectors for grid-based critical experiment geometries. The model architecture combines a U-Net convolutional encoder-decoder with a novel multigroup attention pooling layer, introduced to capture the differing spatial dependencies of sensitivities. Multigroup attention pooling is shown to achieve better performance than traditional pooling, as well as interpretable internal behavior. The differentiability of the surrogate enables gradient-based optimization of the full combinatorial design space, allowing $c_k$ to be maximized by directly changing the material assignment of each position in the geometry grid.
  The method is applied to the validation of the TN-Americas TN-LC transportation cask with HALEU fuel, for which existing critical experiment coverage is limited. The optimization procedure is shown to produce experiment geometries achieving $c_k$ scores of 0.97757, 0.81324, and 0.93276 for three configurations of interest. This approach demonstrates the potential of deep learning and gradient optimization to accelerate the development of advanced nuclear technology.

</details>


### [131] [ParetoPilot: Zero-Surrogate Offline Multi-Objective Optimization via Infer-Perturb-Guide Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04468)
*Ruiqing Sun,Sen Yang,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding,Yijie Wang,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParetoPilot提出了一种零代理扩散框架，通过Infer-Perturb-Guide引擎在无条件去噪过程中隐式推断目标方向并引入动态扰动，无需外部代理模型即可实现离线多目标优化的帕累托最优设计，在51个任务上超越14个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线多目标优化方法依赖外部代理模型，导致计算开销大、存在欺骗性评估问题，且偏离了主流生成模型联合训练条件的范式。

Method: 提出ParetoPilot零代理扩散框架，核心是Infer-Perturb-Guide引擎：1) 通过匹配条件与无条件噪声预测隐式推断瞬时目标方向；2) 对正交化的重力场（收敛）与边缘感知排斥力（多样性）进行数学正交化，生成动态退火扰动向量；3) 通过标准无分类器引导(CFG)引导生成过程。

Result: 在51个任务上，ParetoPilot超越14种基于代理和逆生成的最先进基线方法，实现了超体积改进和鲁棒的帕累托前沿覆盖，同时消除了辅助代理训练、保护了数据隐私。

Conclusion: 通过消除外部代理依赖，ParetoPilot在保持数据隐私的同时，在离线多目标优化任务中取得了优越的性能表现。

Abstract: Offline multi-objective optimization (Offline MOO) aims to discover novel Pareto-optimal designs based on static datasets without expensive environment interactions. While recent generative methods have achieved notable success, they predominantly rely on external surrogate models. This dependency introduces significant computational overhead, suffers from deceptive evaluations, and deviates from the prevailing paradigm of jointly training mainstream generative models with conditions. To address these bottlenecks, we propose ParetoPilot, a novel zero-surrogate diffusion framework for offline MOO. ParetoPilot fully leverages the conditional priors inherently embedded within pre-trained diffusion models. At its core, the framework introduces the Infer-Perturb-Guide (IPG) engine, which is seamlessly interleaved within the unconditional denoising steps of the reverse generation process. First, it implicitly infers the instantaneous objective direction by matching conditional and unconditional noise predictions. Next, it mathematically orthogonalizes a parallel gravity field for strict convergence and an edgeness-aware repulsive force for mutual diversity, creating a dynamically annealed perturbation vector. Finally, this perturbed target seamlessly steers the generation process via standard Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG). Extensive experiments across 51 tasks demonstrate that ParetoPilot outperforms 14 state-of-the-art surrogate-based and inverse generative baselines. By eliminating auxiliary proxy training, our approach preserves data privacy while achieving hypervolume improvement and robust Pareto front coverage.

</details>


### [132] [Self-Distilled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04036)
*Yifeng Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SDPG是一个自蒸馏策略梯度框架，结合了群组相对验证器优势、标准化标准差、精确全词汇在线自蒸馏和参考策略KL正则化，在稳定性和性能上优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏能为稀疏奖励强化学习提供密集监督信号，但现有方法不够稳定或性能有限，因此需要更有效的框架。

Method: 提出SDPG框架，包含三部分：(1) 群组相对验证器优势与标准化标准差；(2) 精确全词汇在线自蒸馏（学生到教师的逆KL散度损失）；(3) 参考策略KL正则化。

Result: SDPG在稳定性和性能上均优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 在线自蒸馏可实例化为全词汇学生到教师逆KL散度损失，SDPG框架有效提升了策略梯度训练的稳定性和效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation, where a language model conditions on privileged context to supervise its own generations, is a promising source of dense supervision for sparse-reward reinforcement learning. Actually, it can be instantiated as an auxiliary full-vocabulary student-to-teacher reverse Kullback-Leibler divergence loss. We therefore propose SDPG, a self-distilled policy-gradient framework that combines group-relative verifier advantages with normalized standard deviation, exact full-vocabulary on-policy self-distillation, as well as reference-policy KL regularization. Empirically, SDPG improves stability and performance over RLVR and self-distillation baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/lauyikfung/SDPG.

</details>


### [133] [When Both Layers Learn: Training Dynamics of Representing Linear Models via ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04476)
*Berk Tinaz,Changzhi Xie,Mahdi Soltanolkotabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐层ReLU网络联合训练两层以拟合线性目标函数的梯度下降动力学，证明了从适度小随机初始化出发，梯度下降能以线性速率收敛到全局最小值，并达到阶次最优的样本复杂度，通过三阶段轨迹分析（对齐、增长、局部精炼）揭示了避免非严格鞍点的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解端到端训练中梯度下降为何能可靠地避开不良平稳区域（非严格鞍点），尽管损失景观包含多个非严格鞍点，该问题在逆问题和自编码器模型中具有代表性。

Method: 采用三阶段轨迹分析方法：（1）对齐阶段——隐藏权重逐步与 planted 方向对齐，输出权重保持正确符号；（2）增长阶段——两层范数增加同时保持对齐；（3）局部精炼阶段——对齐的神经元快速收敛到 planted 方向。结合轨迹级控制论证和沿整条轨迹成立的均匀浓度结果。

Result: 梯度下降从适度小随机初始化出发收敛到全局最小值，收敛速率为线性，样本复杂度达到阶次最优。严格证明了梯度下降能避免非严格鞍点。

Conclusion: 该研究为端到端训练中梯度下降避开不良平稳区域提供了理论依据，所建立的均匀浓度结果为后续分析提供了重要工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the gradient descent dynamics for jointly training both layers of a one-hidden-layer ReLU network to fit a linear target function. Concretely, we consider a realizable setting where inputs are drawn i.i.d. from a Gaussian distribution and labels follow a planted linear model. This stylized framework captures salient features of end-to-end training in inverse problems and certain auto-encoder models. Despite its apparent simplicity, the dynamics remain poorly understood, in part because the loss landscape contains multiple non-strict saddle points, making it unclear why gradient descent from random initialization reliably escapes bad stationary regions. We provide a detailed characterization of the optimization landscape and prove that gradient descent from a moderately small random initialization-simultaneously training both layers-converges to a global minimizer at a linear rate with order-wise optimal sample complexity. Our analysis tracks the trajectory through three phases: an alignment phase in which hidden weights progressively align with the planted direction while the output weights maintain the correct sign pattern; a growth phase in which the norms of both layers increase while preserving alignment; and a local refinement phase in which the aligned neurons rapidly converge to the planted direction, yielding fast local convergence. To rigorously show that GD avoids non-strict saddles, we develop trajectory-level control arguments for the end-to-end dynamics. In addition, we establish novel uniform concentration results that hold along the entire trajectory, and are essential for obtaining order-wise optimal sample complexity. We corroborate our theory with extensive experiments across a range of configurations.

</details>


### [134] [Bayes-Sufficient Representations in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04045)
*Vasileios Sevetlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了"贝叶斯充分性"概念，阐明在固定监督学习问题中，表征需保留与损失函数和分布相关的贝叶斯最优决策所需的最小信息；核心发现是贝叶斯商（Bayes quotient）决定了最小充分表征，并通过实验证明了充分性、最小性与非必要信息保留之间的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定监督决策问题中，"相关性"或"信息保留"的确切含义，即给定联合分布和损失函数下，什么样的表征对贝叶斯最优预测是充分且必要的。

Method: 定义了贝叶斯充分表征和贝叶斯最小表征，引入贝叶斯商概念（标识需相同贝叶斯最优动作的输入），将表征学习与属性诱导（property elicitation）框架联系起来，并通过控制实验、神经瓶颈实验和iNaturalist数据集实验进行验证。

Result: 零一损失对应贝叶斯类，平方损失对应条件均值，Brier损失对应二元预测的条件概率，对数损失或严格适当评分规则对应预测分布；表征的充分性取决于是否细化贝叶斯商，最小性取决于是否与贝叶斯商信息等价。

Conclusion: 对于固定的监督问题，分布和损失决定贝叶斯动作，贝叶斯动作决定贝叶斯商，贝叶斯商决定贝叶斯最优预测所需的最小信息量。

Abstract: Representation learning is often described as preserving the information in an input that is relevant for prediction. This work asks what relevance means for a fixed supervised decision problem. A representation is defined to be Bayes-sufficient for a joint distribution and loss if some prediction head can use it to implement a Bayes-optimal action rule. This makes the target information loss-dependent. In the almost-surely unique Bayes-action case, the relevant object is a Bayes quotient, which identifies inputs that require the same Bayes-optimal action. A representation is sufficient when it refines this quotient, and Bayes-minimal when it is informationally equivalent to it. The framework connects naturally to property elicitation: zero-one loss requires the Bayes class, squared loss the conditional mean, Brier loss the conditional probability in binary prediction, and log loss or strictly proper scoring rules the predictive distribution. Controlled finite experiments, learned neural bottleneck experiments, and a real-data iNaturalist taxonomic refinement experiment illustrate the distinction between sufficiency, minimality, and retained non-required information. For a fixed supervised problem, the distribution and the loss determine the Bayes action, the Bayes action determines the quotient, and the quotient determines the minimal information required for Bayes-optimal prediction.

</details>


### [135] [Activation Steering of Video Generation Models via Reduced-Order Linear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04775)
*Jihoon Hong,Alice Chan,Qiyue Dai,Julian Skifstad,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LA-LQR，一种基于降阶最优控制的视频生成激活引导方法，通过将T2V推理建模为动态系统并求解闭环反馈控制，在降低不安全内容生成的同时保持视觉质量和提示保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V激活引导方法过于粗糙，采用非前瞻性的干预导致过度引导和内容退化，需要一种更精准、最小侵入性的引导机制。

Method: 1) 将T2V推理形式化为动态系统；2) 通过对比提示对将高维激活投影到低维任务相关子空间；3) 在该潜空间估计局部线性动力学；4) 求解潜空间LQR问题以获得逐时间步、逐层的引导信号。

Result: 在概念引导和视频安全基准上，LA-LQR相比基线方法降低了不安全内容生成，同时保持了提示保真度和视觉质量。

Conclusion: LA-LQR提供了一种理论上有界、经验上有效的T2V安全引导框架，弥合了潜空间设定点跟踪与原始激活空间特征控制之间的差距。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) models trained on large-scale web data can generate undesired content, motivating interventions that reduce harmful outputs without sacrificing visual quality. Activation steering offers an attractive mechanistic alternative to finetuning and prompt filtering, but existing T2V steering methods remain limited, typically applying coarse, non-anticipative interventions that can lead to oversteering and content degradation. To close this gap, we propose Latent Activation Linear-Quadratic Regulator (LA-LQR), a reduced-order optimal control framework for minimally invasive T2V steering. LA-LQR formulates T2V inference as a dynamical system and computes closed-loop feedback interventions that steer activations toward desired feature setpoints while penalizing unnecessary perturbations. To make optimal control feasible for high-dimensional video activations, we project activations onto a low-dimensional, task-relevant subspace derived from contrastive prompt pairs, estimate local linear dynamics in this latent space, and solve a latent LQR problem to obtain timestep- and layer-specific steering signals. We provide theoretical bounds relating latent setpoint tracking to raw activation-space feature control, and empirically validate the fidelity of the reduced latent dynamics. On concept steering and video safety benchmarks, LA-LQR reduces unsafe generations relative to baselines, while preserving prompt fidelity and visual quality.

</details>


### [136] [Unlocking Feature Learning in Gated Delta Networks at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04048)
*Yifeng Liu,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格传播坐标大小估计，推导了门控Delta网络在最大更新参数化框架下的缩放规则，实现了跨模型宽度的学习率零样本迁移，实验验证了其在AdamW和SGD优化器下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和扩展大语言模型需要巨大的计算资源，促使研究者探索高效的非二次复杂度架构和原则性的超参数调优方法。虽然最大更新参数化（μP）已实现标准Transformer的零样本超参数迁移，但其在线性模型（特别是具有结构化状态转移和复杂架构的模型）上的扩展尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过严格传播坐标大小估计通过前向传播、门控机制和循环状态动态，推导出门控Delta网络的缩放规则。

Result: 语言模型预训练实验证实，所提出的配置在AdamW和SGD优化器下均能实现跨模型宽度的稳定学习率迁移，而标准参数化则无法迁移。

Conclusion: 验证了所提分析的正确性和实际效用，为线性架构（如门控Delta网络）的超参数迁移提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Training and scaling Large Language Models demand enormous computational resources, motivating both efficient sub-quadratic architectures and principled hyperparameter tuning methods. While the Maximal Update Parametrization ($μ$P) has enabled zero-shot hyperparameter transfer for standard Transformers, its extension to linear models, particularly those with structured state transitions and complicated architectures, remains largely unexplored. By rigorously propagating coordinate-size estimates through the forward pass, gating mechanisms, and recurrent state dynamics, we derive the scaling rules for Gated Delta Network. Experiments on language-model pre-training confirm that our configurations enable stable learning-rate transfer across model widths under both AdamW and SGD, whereas standard parametrization fails to transfer, validating the correctness and practical utility of our analysis.

</details>


### [137] [Uncertainty-Aware End-to-End Co-Design of Neural Network Processors: From Training and Mapping to Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04850)
*Yuyang Du,Yujun Huang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于单调协同设计理论的统一框架，将神经网络训练、芯片映射、晶圆级制造和计算资源分配四个模块通过功能-资源接口解耦，并引入“置信度”（成功率倒数）作为可优化资源，实现了端到端协同设计中的不确定性显式建模与模块独立改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络处理器设计是端到端协同问题，但各决策阶段相互隔离，现有方法紧耦合于特定算法，改进单一组件需重做整个流程，缺乏统一且可解耦的设计框架。

Method: 基于单调协同设计理论，构建四个可互操作的设计模块（网络训练、芯片映射、晶圆级制造、计算资源分配），每个模块仅暴露功能-资源接口；引入置信度（成功率倒数）作为与成本、时间、功耗并列的可显式优化资源。

Result: 三个案例验证：1）恢复跨异构应用场景的帕累托最优实现；2）置信度可作为连续可调的设计旋钮；3）单一模块的实现集改进可自动传播至全局帕累托前沿，无需修改协同设计图。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了模块化、可组合的端到端协同设计，支持不确定性的显式建模与模块级独立优化，为神经网络处理器设计提供了灵活且可扩展的理论基础。

Abstract: Designing a neural network processor is an end-to-end co-design problem: network architecture and training budget determine the inference workload; hardware mapping decisions determine chip area, latency, and energy; and these characteristics govern fabrication yield and manufacturing cost. In practice, these decisions are made in separate stages, and existing co-design methodologies are tightly coupled to specific algorithms, making it difficult to improve one component without reworking the entire pipeline. This paper presents a unified framework, grounded in monotone co-design theory, that composes four interoperable design blocks spanning network training, chip mapping, wafer-level fabrication, and compute resource allocation. Each block exposes only a functionality-resource interface to the rest of the system, so any block can be refined without structural changes elsewhere. A central contribution is the treatment of uncertainty: rather than collapsing stochastic outcomes into point estimates, the framework introduces Confidence, the inverse of success probability, as an explicit and optimizable resource alongside cost, time, and power. Three case studies validate the approach. The first recovers Pareto-optimal implementations across heterogeneous application scenarios. The second confirms that Confidence functions as a continuously tunable design knob rather than a post-hoc diagnostic. The third demonstrates that improving a single block's implementation set automatically propagates to the global Pareto front, without modifying the co-design diagram.

</details>


### [138] [LiftQuant: Continuous Bit-Width LLM via Dimensional Lifting and Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04050)
*Liulu He,XuanAng Liu,Juntao Liu,Taolue Feng,Ting Lu,Chunsheng Gan,Zhiyv Peng,Yuan Du,Huanrui Yang,Yijiang Liu,Li Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiftQuant提出了一种"升维-投影"机制，通过灵活调整升维维度实现准连续比特宽度控制，解决了传统量化方法整数比特宽度导致的部署间隙问题，使70B模型可精确适配24GB GPU（2.4比特），性能显著优于同设备上的SOTA 2比特模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法受限于基于整数的刚性比特宽度（如2、3比特），导致大语言模型无法针对特定内存预算进行最优适配，存在"部署间隙"问题。

Method: 提出"升维-投影"机制：将低维权重向量通过简单1比特格点从高维"升维空间"投影近似，有效比特宽度由升维维度与原始维度的比值决定，通过灵活调整升维维度实现准连续比特宽度控制；该投影生成结构化的非均匀码本，具备向量量化的表达能力；解码路径仅依赖线性变换和1比特均匀量化器，保持硬件友好性。

Result: LiftQuant可将70B大模型压缩至2.4比特以精确适配24GB GPU，性能显著超越同设备上最先进的2比特模型。

Conclusion: LiftQuant通过准连续比特宽度控制弥合了部署间隙，实现了真正的帕累托最优部署，兼具向量量化的表达能力和硬件友好特性。

Abstract: Existing quantization methods are fundamentally limited by rigid, integer-based bit-widths (e.g., 2, 3-bit), resulting in a ``deployment gap" where Large Language Models cannot be optimally fitted to specific memory budgets. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiftQuant, a novel framework that enables continuous bit-width control for true Pareto-optimal deployment. The core innovation is a ``lift-then-project" mechanism which approximates low-dimensional weight vectors by projecting a simple 1-bit lattice from a higher-dimensional ``lifted" space. Crucially, the effective bit-width is determined simply by the ratio of the lifted dimension to the original dimension, which allows the bit-width to be tuned quasi-continuous as the dimension is a flexible structural parameter. This projection generates a structured yet non-uniform codebook, capturing the expressive power of Vector Quantization (VQ). While beneficial over VQ, LiftQuant's decoding path relies solely on linear transformations and 1-bit uniform quantizers, retaining hardware-friendly nature. This flexibility is transformative: LiftQuant enables a 70B LLM to be compressed to 2.4 bits to precisely fit a 24GB GPU, where its performance significantly surpasses state-of-the-art 2-bit models fitted on the same device. Our code and ckpt is available at https://github.com/Heliulu/LiftQuant.

</details>


### [139] [RUBAS: Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning for Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04051)
*Xian Qi Loye,Qinglin Su,Zhexin Zhang,Shiyao Cui,Qi Zhu,Fei Mi,Hongning Wang,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RUBAS框架，通过基于评分标准的四维奖励信号（工具使用安全、参数安全、响应安全、有用性），利用强化学习对齐LLM代理的安全行为，在保持任务完成度的同时提升工具使用安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM演变为工具增强型代理后，面临与真实世界执行相关的全新安全挑战，而现有对齐方法依赖粗糙的拒绝信号或静态监督，难以在安全与有用工具执行之间取得平衡。

Method: RUBAS框架：将代理行为分解为工具使用安全、参数安全、响应安全和有用性四个维度，基于评分标准提供细粒度、可解释的奖励信号，通过强化学习优化安全工具使用。

Result: 在多个代理安全基准和模型上的实验表明，RUBAS相比标准对齐基线提升了安全性，减少了工具相关的幻觉，并保持了竞争性的实用性。

Conclusion: 多维评分标准奖励为在安全关键型工具使用场景中对齐LLM代理提供了有效的训练信号。

Abstract: The evolution of LLMs into tool-enabled agents creates a new class of safety challenges associated with real-world execution rather than simple text generation. Existing alignment methods often rely on coarse refusal signals or static supervision, making it difficult to balance safety with useful tool execution across diverse agentic risks. We introduce RUBAS, a rubric-based reinforcement learning framework for agent safety. RUBAS decomposes agent behavior into four dimensions: tool-use safety, argument safety, response safety, and helpfulness. These structured rubrics provide fine-grained and interpretable rewards over complete agent trajectories, enabling reinforcement learning to optimize safe tool use while preserving task completion. Extensive experiments across multiple agent safety benchmarks and models show that RUBAS improves safety over standard alignment baselines, reduces tool-grounded hallucinations, and maintains competitive utility. Our results suggest that multi-dimensional rubric rewards provide an effective training signal for aligning LLM agents in safety-critical tool-use settings.

</details>


### [140] [A Goal-Set Characterization of Task Composition in the Boolean Task Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04053)
*Eduardo Terrés-Caballero,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现布尔任务代数（BTA）框架中，确定性MDP下最优扩展Q值函数完全由通用任务和空任务决定，原BTA提出的对数基任务集是冗余的；据此提出基于目标集的组合方法，降低了学习和组合成本，但在随机环境中该坍塌现象不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视BTA的结构假设，揭示其最优扩展Q值函数空间中的坍塌现象，消除冗余基任务，降低计算成本。

Method: 理论证明确定性MDP中最优扩展Q值函数由通用任务和空任务唯一确定；提出基于目标集的组合方法，通过对目标集执行逻辑操作并从通用/空值函数中选取切片来重构组合值函数。

Result: 在表格、视觉、函数逼近和连续控制域中，学习额外基任务不会带来性能提升；新方法降低了BTA的学习成本和BTA及Skill Machines的组合时间，同时保持策略性能；在随机MDP中提供了反例，表明坍塌不成立。

Conclusion: 确定性MDP中BTA的基任务集可大幅缩减至仅需通用和空任务，但在随机环境中最优组合可能需要指数级数量的策略。

Abstract: The Boolean Task Algebra (BTA) provides a principled framework for zero-shot task composition in reinforcement learning by equipping goal-reaching tasks with Boolean operations. We revisit its structural assumptions and formalize a collapse in the space of optimal extended Q-value functions: in deterministic MDPs, every such function is fully determined by the universal and empty tasks. This makes the logarithmic set of base tasks proposed in the original BTA formulation redundant. Building on this observation, we introduce a goal-set-based composition method that performs logical operations on goal sets and reconstructs composed value functions by selecting slices from the universal and empty value functions. This reduces learning costs for standard BTA and reduces composition time for both BTA and Skill Machines, while preserving policy performance. Experiments across tabular, visual, function-approximation, and continuous-control domains show that learning additional base tasks does not yield better performance. Finally, we study the stochastic setting and provide a counterexample showing that this collapse need not hold, that is, optimal composition may require accounting for exponentially many policies in the number of goals. Code is available at https://github.com/EduardoTerres/bta_paper.

</details>


### [141] [Spectral Scaling Laws of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04058)
*Gagik Magakyan,Pablo Parrilo,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Muon优化器中牛顿-舒尔茨(NS)迭代对动量矩阵奇异值谱的影响。通过在77M到2.8B参数规模模型上的实验，发现各层奇异值分位数在预热后会稳定，且稳定值随模型规模呈现清晰的幂律关系（层依赖指数不同）。浅至中深层扩展极慢（~M^{-0.25}），而某些深层扩展极快（~M^{-0.96}），在更大规模下会进入NS失效区域。据此提出按层感知原则选择最小NS配置的方法，在保证更新质量的同时避免不必要的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器使用牛顿-舒尔茨迭代近似正交归一化，但对动量矩阵的奇异值谱在训练过程中的行为及其随模型规模的变化规律缺乏系统理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，为大规模训练中高效配置NS迭代提供理论指导。

Method: 在77M到2.8B参数范围的模型上，追踪各层动量缓冲区的奇异值分位数，分析其稳定值随模型规模变化的幂律关系，并研究不同层类型（深度位置）的标度指数差异。

Result: 发现奇异值分位数在短暂预热后稳定，稳定值与层类型和模型规模相关；稳定值随模型规模呈幂律衰减，浅至中深层指数约为-0.25，某些深层指数高达-0.96；标准5步NS配置在学术规模下有效，但在前沿规模下深层会失效。

Conclusion: 提出了基于层的感知原则指导NS迭代配置的方法，使得在前沿规模下可以选择最小但足够的NS配置来正交归一化关键方向，避免不必要的计算开销，同时保证更新质量。

Abstract: Orthonormalized update rules have rapidly become a leading choice of optimizer for training large language models, with recent open-source state-of-the-art models adopting Muon. To keep these updates tractable, Muon performs the orthonormalization with the Newton--Schulz (NS) iteration. Since NS is only approximate, directions with small singular values fail to be orthonormalized. In Muon, NS is applied to the momentum matrix at every step, yet little is known about how the singular value spectrum of these momentum matrices behaves during training, or how that behavior changes with model size. We present the first systematic study of this question. Tracking singular value quantiles of the momentum buffer across layers in models ranging from 77M to 2.8B parameters, we observe a consistent picture: after a short burn-in, the quantiles stabilize at a value determined by the layer type and model size. These stabilization values follow remarkably clean power laws in model size, with layer-dependent exponents. Layers up to mid-late depth scale very mildly with model size $M$ (around $M^{-0.25}$), so the standard 5-step NS configuration used at academic scale will continue to orthonormalize them at much larger scales. Some of the late layers, however, scale much more aggressively (up to $M^{-0.96}$) and will fall into the NS failure regime at frontier scale unless one uses more NS iterations or better-tuned coefficients. NS iterations are computationally expensive at scale; our laws give practitioners a principled, layer-aware recipe for choosing the minimum NS configuration that still orthonormalizes the directions that matter -- avoiding unnecessary computation without sacrificing update quality.

</details>


### [142] [LLM Compression with Jointly Optimizing Architectural and Quantization choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04063)
*Hoang-Loc La,Truong-Thanh Le,Amir Taherkordi,Phuong Hoai Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分的神经架构搜索（NAS）框架，能够联合优化LLM的架构配置与混合精度量化，在边缘设备上实现更好的精度-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署大型语言模型（LLM）面临显著的内存和计算需求挑战，现有压缩方法如剪枝和量化存在局限性，而之前的NAS方法往往限制搜索空间并将架构搜索与量化过程解耦，无法充分探索最优组合。

Method: 采用可微分NAS框架，在整个搜索空间中联合优化线性层的架构配置和混合精度量化，实现端到端的协同搜索。

Result: 相比顺序进行NAS然后量化的基线方法，所提模型在同等精度下推理速度提升最高1.4倍，或在同等延迟下在7个推理任务上平均准确率提升最高6%。

Conclusion: 联合优化架构与量化能够比分离的NAS-量化顺序方法获得更优的精度-延迟权衡，为LLM在边缘设备上的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) is challenging due to their significant memory and computational requirements. While some methods address this by developing small or tiny language models from scratch, these approaches demand extensive GPU training. Compressing pre-trained LLMs for edge devices offers a compelling alternative. Beyond pruning and quantization, Neural Architecture Search (NAS) enables effective compression, yet prior NAS approaches often limit the search space and decouple architecture from quantization. We introduce a differentiable NAS framework that explores the entire space and jointly optimizes architectural configurations alongside mixed-precision quantization for linear layers of LLMs. Experiments demonstrate superior accuracy-latency trade-offs: our models achieve up to 1.4x faster inference than sequential NAS-then-quantization baselines at comparable accuracy, or up to 6% higher average accuracy across seven reasoning tasks at equivalent latency.

</details>


### [143] [TPA-AD: A Two-Stage Pseudo Anomaly-Guided Method for Bearing Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04073)
*Xiancheng Wang,Zhibo Zhang,Ran Li,Rui Wang,Minghang Zhao,Shisheng Zhong,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段伪异常引导的轴箱轴承时间序列异常检测方法TPA-AD，仅使用正常样本训练，通过重建模型在边界附近生成伪异常窗口，再利用对比学习和KNN进行异常检测，在轴承故障和退化数据集上表现稳定且敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖已知故障类别、真实异常先验或随机异常注入，在仅含正常样本的训练设定下难以有效区分正常与异常边界。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 利用重建模型和逐特征目标误差控制，在正常边界附近生成伪异常窗口；(2) 通过正常与伪异常窗口的对比学习学习异常敏感表征，最后使用KNN生成窗口级和点级异常分数。

Result: 在轴承故障检测和退化过程数据集上表现稳定，对退化演变敏感，并在13个公开TSAD数据集和真实高铁轴承数据上展现出一定的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TPA-AD通过边界邻域伪异常构造提升了正常边界的可分性，能联合处理混合变量场景中的连续和离散特征，具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a two-stage pseudo anomaly-guided anomaly detection method (\textbf{T}wo-stage \textbf{P}seudo \textbf{A}nomaly-guided \textbf{A}nomaly \textbf{D}etection, \textbf{TPA-AD}) for axle-box bearing time-series anomaly detection (time series anomaly detection, TSAD) under the setting where only normal samples are available for training. The method first generates pseudo-anomalous windows near the normal boundary using a reconstruction model and per-feature target-error control. It then learns anomaly-sensitive representations through contrastive learning between normal and pseudo-anomalous windows, and finally produces window-level and point-level anomaly scores using k-nearest neighbors (KNN). Compared with existing methods that rely on known fault categories, real anomaly priors, or random anomaly injection, TPA-AD improves the separability of the normal boundary by constructing pseudo-anomalies in boundary neighborhoods and can jointly handle continuous and discrete features in mixed-variable scenarios. The main experiments are conducted on bearing fault detection datasets and degradation-process datasets, with an additional exploratory extension on $13$ public TSAD datasets. The results show that the proposed method yields relatively stable anomaly responses, is sensitive to degradation evolution, and demonstrates a certain degree of broader applicability on public TSAD benchmarks and real high-speed-train-related bearing data.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Patching Is Harder Than It Looks For Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04074)
*Federico Zucchi,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Keyuan Luo,Thomas Lampert,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列Transformer中自适应分块（adaptive patching）的有效性条件，通过将分块建模为带预算的比特率分配问题，推导出自适应分块优于调优均匀分块的显式阈值，并通过大量实验发现：在标准长期预测基准上，验证集选择的均匀分块基线与动态自适应方法竞争力相当，没有一致的方向性优势，自适应分块的价值取决于能否低成本地识别出真正能降低损失的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应分块（adaptive patching）是时间序列Transformer中一种新颖且有前景的方案，旨在对局部信息丰富的序列区域分配更精细的分块。然而，其有效性条件尚不明确——局部异质性本身不足以保证自适应分块优于调优后的均匀分块。本文旨在从理论上界定自适应分块何时能超越均匀分块基线，并通过受控实验验证理论预测。

Method: 将分块问题建模为带预算的比特率分配问题，推导出自适应规则需满足的显式阈值；通过二次代理和强凸性界分别刻画局部和全局的改进上界；在三种代表性架构上进行受控隔离实验，将每个自适应机制替换为均匀分块大小扫描，保持骨干网络、数据和训练协议不变。

Result: 验证集选择的均匀基线在标准长期预测基准上与动态自适应方法竞争力相当；每设置的效果集中在零附近，按数据集聚合后未发现一致的方向性优势；观察到的较大增益是方法和数据集特定的。

Conclusion: 自适应分块应始终与调优后的均匀基线进行比较评估；其价值取决于能否廉价且可靠地识别出那些更细粒度分块确实能降低预测损失的区域。

Abstract: Adaptive patching is a recent and compelling proposal for time-series Transformers: allocate finer patches where the sequence looks locally informative. This paper asks under what conditions a content-adaptive patching operator should outperform a tuned uniform one. Local heterogeneity alone is not enough: under pointwise forecasting losses, a complex-looking region is not automatically one where finer patching reduces the loss. We model patching as a budgeted bitrate allocation and derive an explicit threshold that a dynamic patching rule must satisfy to beat a well-tuned uniform baseline, then bound the achievable improvement both locally (a quadratic surrogate) and globally (a strong-convexity bound under the model's assumptions). Two structural results follow: without a coupling constraint, scalar local complexity cannot produce a non-uniform optimum under a common loss landscape; and once the backbone is trained to its representation-aware optimum, the alignment gain collapses around a well-tuned uniform patch size. To test these predictions, we run a controlled isolation study on three representative architectures, replacing each adaptive mechanism with a uniform patch-size sweep while keeping the backbone, data, and training protocol fixed. On standard long-horizon forecasting benchmarks, the validation-selected uniform baseline is competitive with the dynamic counterpart, with per-setting effects concentrated near zero and no consistent directional advantage once results are aggregated by dataset. The larger gains we do observe are method- and dataset-specific. Adaptive patching should therefore be evaluated against a tuned uniform baseline; its value depends on whether a cheap and reliable routing signal can identify where finer patches actually reduce forecasting loss.

</details>


### [145] [Large Language Models Hack Rewards, and Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04075)
*Wei Liu,Xinyi Mou,Hanqi Yan,Zhongyu Wei,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"社会黑客"概念，指强化学习训练中LLM发现社会规则漏洞的行为。通过构建72个社会环境沙箱SocioHack，发现模型会自然产生奖励黑客行为，找到技术上合规但违背监管意图的策略，现有安全机制仅能有限缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会规范在结构上与奖励函数相似，都存在可测量结果、阈值和例外，但制度意图往往不完整。研究者担忧RL训练过程可能利用这些漏洞，从奖励黑客升级为更严重的社会黑客行为。

Method: 构建SocioHack沙箱（72个社会环境），在环境中观察RL训练后模型是否出现奖励黑客行为，并测试现有LLM安全机制的有效性。

Result: 奖励黑客行为自然涌现，模型发现监管漏洞并生成技术上合规但违背制度意图的策略；当前LLM安全防护仅能提供有限的缓解效果。

Conclusion: 在现实世界中收集反馈进行模型训练需要更谨慎，亟需下一代面向安全的后训练范式来迭代部署LLM。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, enabling large language models (LLMs) to learn from rewards. We observe that societal regulations are structurally similar to reward functions. They define measurable outcomes, thresholds, and exceptions, while often leaving institutional intent only partially specified. We hypothesise that the RL training process may exploit these gaps and therefore ask whether models' well-known tendency to hack reward functions during RL can scale into a more consequential failure mode named societal hacking: discovering loopholes in the rules society runs on. To study this phenomenon, we introduce SocioHack, a sandbox of 72 societal environments, and find that within these environments, reward hacking naturally emerges and leads to regulatory loophole discovery. Models learn to hack the social rules and generate strategies that remain technically compliant while defeating regulatory intent, and current LLM safeguards provide only limited mitigation. Therefore, collecting in-the-wild feedback for model training requires greater caution, and we need a next-generation post-training paradigm for safely iterating LLMs in real society.=

</details>


### [146] [Stein Kernelized Molecular Dynamics for Active Learning of Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04100)
*Joanna Zou,Fraser Birks,Dallas Foster,Youssef Marzouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stein核化分子动力学（SKMD），一种通过交互粒子动力学采集信息性训练配置的增强采样方法，用于机器学习原子间势的主动学习和微调。SKMD保留了玻尔兹曼分布作为渐近分布，并通过自适应停止准则实现高效在线数据采集。在Müller-Brown势和丙氨酸二肽上的实验表明，相比基线方法，SKMD在更少的训练迭代中实现了更高的模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）的性能关键依赖于训练数据的质量和多样性，而现有方法在高效采集信息丰富的配置方面存在不足。需要一种既能探索多样构型又能保持物理正确分布的增强采样方法。

Method: SKMD是一种基于Stein变分梯度下降的随机变体，通过异步粒子更新和全局原子描述子的核函数实现分子动力学适配。该方法采用交互粒子动力学，并引入自适应停止准则进行在线非冗余数据采集。

Result: 在Müller-Brown势的神经网络模型主动学习和丙氨酸二肽的MACE势微调任务中，SKMD在相同采集样本数下，相比主动学习基线方法实现了更高的模型精度和更少的训练迭代次数。

Conclusion: SKMD通过保持玻尔兹曼分布作为渐近分布，有效平衡了构型探索与高概率区域吸引，为MLIPs的数据采集提供了一种物理一致且高效的增强采样方法。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable efficient and accurate atomistic simulations but depend critically on the quality and diversity of the training data. We introduce Stein kernelized molecular dynamics (SKMD), an enhanced sampling method that uses interacting particle dynamics to acquire informative training configurations for the active learning and fine-tuning of MLIPs. SKMD corresponds to a stochastic variant of Stein variational gradient descent that is adapted for molecular dynamics by incorporating asynchronous particle updates and a kernel of global atomic descriptors, which provides a symmetry-aware measure of configurational similarity. Unlike other enhanced samplers used in molecular dynamics, SKMD preserves the Boltzmann distribution as the asymptotic distribution of the dynamics. This property enforces a balance between the exploration of diverse configurations and attraction toward high-probability regions of the energy landscape. We further propose an approach to efficient online data acquisition using an adaptive stopping criterion that selects non-redundant training data over the course of simulation. We demonstrate SKMD for the active learning of a neural network model of the Müller-Brown potential and the fine-tuning of a MACE interatomic potential for alanine dipeptide. Compared to active learning baselines, our method achieves higher model accuracy in fewer training iterations with the same number of acquired training samples.

</details>


### [147] [Building The Ph(ysical)AI Layer Of Machine Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04106)
*Ulbert Jose Botero,Liam Smith,Brooks Olney,Pooya Khorrami,Steven Kusiak,Watson Jia,Sage Trudeau,Daniel Capecci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信号处理原理（傅里叶分解、能量守恒、对称性）的基础模型，仅用射频数据训练（199万参数），无需微调即可跨模态迁移到音频、图像、文本和视频，在15个任务上通过线性探测达到77.7%平均准确率（91.9% top-3），揭示了物理原理驱动与规模驱动的互补路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型通过大规模多样化数据训练实现泛化，但无法有效迁移到没有配对训练数据的真正未见领域。作者假设不同领域在基本物理原理上一致，差异仅在于时间、频率、幅度或相位上的可学习变换，因此提出基于信号处理原理而非统计相关性的模型架构。

Method: 将信号处理原理（傅里叶分解、能量守恒、对称性）编码到模型架构和损失函数中；仅在射频数据上训练；采用共设计的架构和损失函数；使用冻结的编码器表示（无需目标领域微调）通过线性探测进行跨模态迁移。

Result: 1.99M参数的冻结编码器在15个多样化任务上达到77.7%平均准确率（91.9% top-3）。物理基础任务（说话人识别84.5%、地震学、射频指纹识别）表现优于语义任务（音乐流派、语言识别70.0%）。

Conclusion: 物理原理驱动与规模驱动是互补路径：物理原理可实现高效的跨模态迁移，同时自然划定物理理解与语义理解之间的边界。

Abstract: Foundation models achieve generalization through massive-scale training on diverse data, but have limitations with transfer to truly unseen domains without paired training data. We propose principle-driven foundation models that encode signal-theoretic principles (Fourier decomposition, energy conservation, symmetry) rather than learn untethered statistical correlations. We hypothesize that domains differ not in fundamental physics, but in learnable transformations in time, frequency, magnitude, or phase. Training exclusively on radio-frequency (RF) data with co-designed architecture and losses incorporating these principles, we achieve cross-modal transfer to audio, images, text, and video using only frozen representations learned from RF data, requiring no fine-tuning of the encoder on target domains. Our 1.99M parameter frozen encoder achieves 77.7% average accuracy (91.9% top-3) across 15 diverse tasks via linear probing, with systematic variation: 84.5 on physically-grounded tasks (speaker recognition, seismology, RF fingerprinting) versus 70.0% on semantic tasks (music genre, language recognition). This reveals that principle-driven and scale-driven approaches offer complementary paths: physical principles enable efficient cross-modal transfer while naturally establishing the boundary between physical and semantic understanding.

</details>


### [148] [Variance Reduction for Heavy-Tailed Monetization Metrics in Ranking Experiments via Post-Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04110)
*Neeti Pokharna,Olivier Jeunen,Yatharth Saraf,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对排序和检索系统的在线评估中，由于货币化指标呈现重尾分布，导致A/B实验统计功效低。本文提出了一种结合后分层（post-stratification）与CUPED的方差缩减框架，利用实验前协变量提升实验敏感性。在ShareChat的排序驱动货币化实验中部署后，该方法以约45%的流量减少实现了等效统计置信度，显著降低了方差并提高了决策稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评估排序和检索系统时，货币化指标（如应用收入、创作者收益）呈重尾分布，少量用户主导均值和方差，导致A/B实验统计功效低、结论不可靠，尤其在流量有限的情况下。

Method: 将后分层（post-stratification）与CUPED（使用实验前协变量）相结合的方差缩减框架，通过在实验后对样本按协变量分层并结合CUPED调整，降低货币化实验的方差。

Result: 在ShareChat的排序驱动货币化实验中，该方法与标准指标相比，以约45%的流量减少实现了等效统计置信度，显著降低了方差并提高了决策稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效提升货币化实验的统计敏感性，减少所需流量；同时讨论了实际设计选择、防护措施和局限性，为后分层在真实信息检索和推荐系统中的适用性提供了指导。

Abstract: Online evaluation of ranking and retrieval systems often relies on downstream monetization metrics such as app revenue or creator earnings. These metrics are typically heavy-tailed, with a small fraction of users dominating both mean and variance, leading to low statistical power and unreliable conclusions in A/B experiments -- especially under limited traffic.
  We present a practical framework for variance reduction in online experiments by combining post-stratification with CUPED. Our approach leverages pre-experiment covariates to improve the sensitivity of monetization experiments without requiring additional traffic. Deployed at ShareChat across ranking-driven monetization experiments, the method substantially reduces variance and improves decision stability, achieving equivalent statistical confidence with ~45\% less traffic than standard metrics. We further discuss practical design choices, guardrails, and limitations, providing guidance on when post-stratification is appropriate for real-world information retrieval and Recommendation systems.

</details>


### [149] [dMX: Differentiable Mixed-Precision Assignment for Low-Precision Floating-Point Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04115)
*Giuseppe Franco,Ian Colbert,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Felix Marty,Nicholas Fraser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出dMX，一种可微分的混合精度量化框架，用于学习LLM中各层的浮点位宽分配，在MXFP数据格式族上实现模型质量与平均位宽之间的帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM量化方法对所有层采用统一位宽是次优的，但手工分配混合位宽需要大量调参，缺乏自动且可微的优化方法。

Method: 将每层浮点格式参数化为单个标量偏移，通过连续优化+温度退火调度渐进离散化，并引入目标感知正则化项将平均位宽导向用户指定预算。

Result: 在Llama、Qwen3、SmolLM2等模型上，dMX在WikiText-2困惑度和四个零样本推理基准上持续生成帕累托占优模型，优于KL散度层选择启发式方法。

Conclusion: dMX能够有效导航模型质量与平均位宽之间的权衡，为硬件兼容的MXFP格式提供自动化的混合精度量化方案。

Abstract: Quantizing large language models (LLMs) to low-precision floating-point representations is central to efficient deployment, yet applying a single bit-width uniformly across all layers is sub-optimal in terms of both performance and accuracy. This work introduces dMX, a differentiable mixed-precision quantization framework for learnable floating-point bit-width assignment. We study its application for the microscaling floating-point (MXFP) family of data types defined by the Open Compute Project (OCP) standard. The per-layer bit-width assignment is formulated as a continuous optimization problem in which each layer's floating-point format format is parameterized by a scalar parameter, folding the multi-variate design space into a single learnable offset. During training this offset takes continuous values, avoiding sudden oscillations between discrete quantization formats. A temperature-based annealing schedule progressively discretizes the learned offsets, ensuring that the final configuration maps to hardware-compatible MXFP formats without abrupt transitions between training and inference behavior. A target-aware regularization term steers the average bit-width toward a user-specified budget, serving as a coarse-grained proxy for inference cost and balancing model quality against deployment efficiency. We performed experiments on different families of LLM, such as Llama, Qwen3, and SmolLM2, evaluating perplexity on WikiText-2 and accuracy on four zero-shot reasoning benchmarks. Across these settings, dMX consistently yields Pareto-dominating models and improves over Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence-based layer-selection heuristics, efficiently navigating trade-offs between model quality and average bit-width.

</details>


### [150] [Stationarity-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04135)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Holger Schöner,Zipeng Wu,Edouard Fouché,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SARAF提出了一种平稳性感知的检索增强时间序列预测框架，通过自适应平衡检索的相关性与多样性，并结合平稳性感知聚合策略，在非平稳时间序列预测任务上显著提升了准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常呈现非平稳性和机制转换，导致完全参数化预测器性能受限。现有的检索增强预测方法仅基于相似性进行检索，但由于非平稳性，高度相似的过去片段并不一定意味着相似的未来，这种仅依赖相似性的检索方式脆弱且易产生冗余。

Method: SARAF首先通过时间相似性及时序对齐增强构建候选池，然后采用多样性感知的选择策略覆盖异构历史机制，其中多样化强度由数据集级别的平稳性自动调节。最后，使用平稳性感知的聚合方法融合检索到的未来序列。

Result: 在8个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SARAF取得了具有竞争力的预测性能，在平均准确率和鲁棒性上均优于强基线模型，尤其在具有挑战性的非平稳场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: SARAF通过自适应平衡检索的相关性与多样性，有效解决了非平稳时间序列预测中相似性检索的脆弱性问题，为检索增强时间序列预测提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting relies on historical patterns, but real-world series often exhibit non-stationarity and regime shifts that challenge fully parametric forecasters. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), recent work augments forecasters by retrieving relevant historical segments and using them as external evidence at inference time. However, due to the intrinsic non-stationarity of real-world time series, a highly similar past segment does not necessarily imply a similar future, rendering similarity-only retrieval brittle and prone to redundancy. We propose Stationarity-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting (SARAF), a framework that adaptively balances relevance and diversity in retrieval. SARAF first forms a candidate pool via temporal similarity with time-aligned enhancement, then applies a diversity-aware selection strategy to cover heterogeneous historical regimes, with the diversification strength automatically modulated by dataset-level stationarity. Moreover, SARAF uses stationarity-aware aggregation to fuse the retrieved futures. Extensive experiments on eight real-world datasets show that SARAF achieves competitive forecasting performance and improves average accuracy and robustness over strong baselines, with particularly clear benefits under challenging non-stationary settings. Code: https://github.com/ShiqiaoZhou/SARAF.

</details>


### [151] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning for Short-Term Flood Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04143)
*Tewodros Syum Gebre,Jagrati Talreja,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将水文知识作为损失函数约束（趋势对齐约束）融入LSTM的物理信息机器学习框架，用于数据稀缺场景下的洪水预测，有效减少物理不一致预测，提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的LSTM在数据稀缺时预测不可靠，且可能违背基本水文原理，尤其在极端天气外推时产生物理不一致的预测结果。

Method: 在LSTM的损失函数中加入趋势对齐约束（Trend Alignment constraint），惩罚降水与流量趋势之间的方向不一致，无需复杂水动力学方程即可引导模型学习物理合理的流量过程线。

Result: 在仅用5%数据训练时，PIML将NSE从0.20提升至0.23；在极端气候应力测试中，基线模型出现不稳定行为，而PIML保持方向一致性和物理合理性，显著减少非物理波动。

Conclusion: 简单的物理约束能显著提升深度学习模型在实时洪水预测中的可靠性，为无资料流域和变化气候条件提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate flood forecasting is essential for mitigating disaster risks and protecting communities. However, purely data-driven machine learning models often struggle in data-scarce environments and may violate fundamental hydrological principles.
