Standard Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can generate physically inconsistent predictions, particularly when extrapolating to extreme weather conditions. To address these limitations, we propose a Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) framework that incorporates hydrological knowledge directly into the loss function of an LSTM model. Specifically, a Trend Alignment constraint penalizes directional inconsistencies between precipitation and discharge trends, improving model robustness without requiring complex hydrodynamic equations. This regularization encourages the model to learn physically plausible hydrograph behavior, even with limited training data, while enhancing reliability during peak flood events. Experimental results show that the proposed physics-informed model outperforms a standard LSTM baseline in data-scarce settings, increasing the Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency (NSE) from 0.20 to 0.23 when trained on only 5% of the available data. Additional stress tests under simulated extreme climate scenarios demonstrate that the baseline model exhibits unstable behavior, whereas the physics-informed model maintains directional consistency and physical plausibility. Although accurately predicting extreme peak magnitudes remains challenging with limited data, the proposed approach substantially reduces unphysical fluctuations common in purely data-driven models. These findings demonstrate that simple physical constraints can significantly improve the reliability of deep learning models for real-time flood forecasting, offering a practical solution for ungauged basins and evolving climate conditions.

</details>


### [152] [EvalStop: Using World Feedback to Detect and Correct Reward Overoptimization in Multi-Tenant RLHF Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04145)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Shahryar Sarkani,John M. Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalStop，一种基于评估分数连续下降来终止RLHF训练任务的可组合调度原语，在检测奖励黑客行为时达到98%精度/99%召回率，同时提升JCT 9%并减少22%的算力浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云LLM微调平台上的RLHF工作负载存在奖励过度优化问题（奖励模型代理指标与真实世界反馈偏离），现有调度器（非clairvoyant调度器忽略质量信号、SLAQ式调度器使用单调下降的训练损失作为弱代理、经典early stopping需人工监控且不释放共享GPU）均无法有效检测并终止此类奖励黑客行为。

Method: 提出EvalStop调度原语：监控评估分数连续k次下降作为终止信号，释放GPU、保留最佳检查点，可组合到任意基础调度器上。将调度器级early stopping建模为检测问题，在离散事件模拟器中评估，混合了奖励黑客和结构健康运行的RLHF工作负载，真实标签对调度器隐藏。

Result: 在80% RLHF、64 GPU的负载下：精度98%、召回99%、FPR 1.5%；相比SRTF-Est提升JCT 9%、减少浪费计算22%（p<0.05）。固定进度和损失平局基线方法要么在健康RLHF上产生65% FPR，要么漏检半数以上黑客案例。所有基础调度器上均获得9-25% JCT提升；评估噪声标准差≤0.05时精度≥91%；黑客比例20-80%时精度≥89%。

Conclusion: EvalStop作为一种轻量级、可组合的调度原语，能有效检测RLHF中的奖励黑客行为，同时提升调度效率，在不同基础调度器、噪声水平和黑客比例下均保持稳定的检测质量。

Abstract: Cloud LLM fine-tuning platforms increasingly serve RLHF workloads, where a learned reward model is optimized as a proxy for human quality. As Gao et al. (2023) showed, this proxy diverges from world feedback (downstream eval metrics) under sustained optimization pressure, a phenomenon known as reward overoptimization. Existing platform schedulers ignore this divergence: non-clairvoyant schedulers optimize JCT without any quality signal, SLAQ-style quality-aware schedulers use training loss (a weaker proxy that drops monotonically through hacking), and classical per-job early stopping requires human monitoring and does not free shared GPUs. We propose EvalStop, a composable scheduling primitive that terminates jobs on k consecutive eval-score declines, releases GPUs, preserves the best checkpoint, and delegates to any base scheduler. We frame scheduler-level early stopping as a detection problem and evaluate it in a discrete-event simulator whose RLHF workload mixes reward-hacking and structurally healthy runs, with ground-truth labels hidden from schedulers. On RLHF-heavy workloads (80% RLHF, 64 GPUs), EvalStop achieves precision 98% / recall 99% / FPR 1.5% while improving JCT by 9% and cutting wasted compute by 22% over SRTF-Est (p<0.05). Trivial fixed-progress and loss-plateau competitors either incur 65% FPR on healthy RLHF or miss over half of true hacking cases. Gains compose across every base scheduler tested (9-25% JCT) and detection quality stays stable under eval noise (precision at least 91% at noise std <= 0.05) and hacking base rate (precision at least 89% across 20-80% hacking fractions).

</details>


### [153] [When Offline Selectors Cannot Beat the Best Single Model: A Diagnostic Study on edX Dropout Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04161)
*Tyler Crosse,Alan Nadelsticher Ruvalcaba,Dustin Khang LeDuc,Thomas Trask,Nicholas Lytle,David Joyner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段诊断工具，用于区分离线选择器（如BC、DQN、CQL）在实例级模型选择中失败的三种原因：学习器不匹配、状态设计不足、或标签偏移。在edX辍学预测数据上应用发现，瓶颈是局部表示模糊性，而非保守性或偏移，建议改进状态或收集新数据而非调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线训练的选择器在实际部署中常无法超越单一最强模型，但根本原因（学习器问题、状态设计问题、标签偏移）常混杂在一起未被分离，导致盲目调参效率低下。

Method: 三阶段诊断：Stage 1 通过k-NN标签一致性估计局部上限；Stage 2 检验BC、DQN、CQL等离线学习器是否达到该上限；Stage 3 消融选择器状态，测试更丰富的特征能否提升上限。

Result: 在edX辍学预测任务中，Oracle比最强单模型平均高出9.7个准确率点，但BC、DQN、CQL均落在同一较低准确率区间；瓶颈是局部表示模糊性（CQL缩小了模仿差距但无部署增益，三种学习器测试准确率收敛），而非保守性或标签偏移。

Conclusion: 该诊断框架可有效定位离线选择器失败根因；当前最有效的下一步是改进状态设计或收集新数据，而非继续调优离线学习器。

Abstract: Different predictors often excel on different inputs, so picking the best one per instance promises higher accuracy than committing to a single model. In practice, selectors trained from logged data routinely fail to beat the strongest single predictor. Three causes typically go unseparated before more tuning is applied: a mismatched learner, a state that does not predict which model wins, or buffer-to-deployment label shift.
  A three-stage diagnostic rules them out on a shared buffer. Stage~1 estimates a local ceiling on oracle recovery from $k$-NN label consistency. Stage~2 asks whether paired BC and offline-RL learners (BC, DQN, and CQL across penalty weights) reach that ceiling. Stage~3 ablates the selector state to test whether richer features would raise it. The combined verdict points to the most promising next step: tuning the learner, redesigning the state, or collecting new data.
  We apply it to selecting among five dropout-prediction models on edX clickstream data. Across 16 windows, the oracle beats the strongest single base model by 9.7 accuracy points on average, yet BC, DQN, and CQL land in the same test-accuracy band below it (robust to a tenfold buffer sweep and $N{=}2{,}000$ held-out examples). The bottleneck is local representational ambiguity: CQL closes the imitation gap without a deployment gain (not conservatism), regret clusters tightly across learners (not tie-breaking), and the three learners converge on test accuracy (not shift). The next iteration should change the state or collect new data, not tune the offline learner further.

</details>


### [154] [ADAPTOOD: Uncertainty-Aware Fine-Tuning for Out-of-Distribution ECG Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04164)
*Sotirios Vavaroutas,Yu Yvonne Wu,Ali Etemad,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADAPTOOD框架，通过数据不确定性量化分布偏移严重程度，指导时间序列模型的微调，在OOD任务上比现有方法提升高达7%准确率和12.9%精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设固定的分布偏移，无法应对多种类型或严重程度的偏移；且忽视偏移严重性，例如将适应大型熟悉数据集与适应小型新任务数据集等同处理，导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 利用数据不确定性量化目标部署分布样本与预训练分布之间的偏差程度，作为OOD严重性的直接信号；结合低秩模型更新和自适应超参数优化来改进微调过程。

Result: 在OOD任务上，ADAPTOOD比现有方法准确率提升高达7%，精确率提升高达12.9%，且在分布偏移严重程度增加时仍保持强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性感知的微调框架，有效应对时间序列中多种类型和严重程度的分布偏移，提升了模型在真实场景中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Data samples used for training often differ from those encountered during fine-tuning and deployment, and while ML models show promise, their performance remains limited when only small annotated datasets are available. Performance often degrades under distribution shifts caused by diverse sensors, populations, and application settings. Although pre-training helps, models frequently encounter out-of-distribution (OOD) data in real-world settings, leading to reduced robustness. Existing adaptation methods usually assume fixed distribution shifts and struggle when multiple types or severities occur. In particular, they overlook shift severity, for example treating adaptation to a large familiar dataset the same as adaptation to a small dataset with a new task, which limits generalisation. To address this, we propose ADAPTOOD, a novel framework that leverages data uncertainty to quantify distribution shift severity and guide fine-tuning for time series. This uncertainty measures how strongly samples from the target deployment distribution deviate from the pre-training distribution, providing a direct signal of OOD severity. Our framework combines this uncertainty with low-rank model updates and adaptive hyperparameter optimisation to improve adaptation. We show that ADAPTOOD achieves up to 7% higher accuracy and 12.9% higher precision than existing methods in OOD tasks, maintaining strong performance as distribution shift severity increases.

</details>


### [155] [Smart Transportation Without Neurons -- Fair Metro Network Expansion with Tabular Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04167)
*Dimitris Michailidis,Sennay Ghebreab,Fernando P. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明地铁网络扩展问题（MNEP）规模足够小，无需使用深度强化学习（Deep RL），通过将其重新表述为非马尔可夫奖励决策过程（NMRDP）并采用表格型强化学习（tabular RL），在显著减少训练轮次和碳排放的同时，达到与Deep RL相近的性能，并提升了可解释性。在西安和阿姆斯特丹的真实场景中，训练轮次减少18倍，碳排放减少12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决地铁网络扩展问题（MNEP）的方法中，精确算法和启发式方法依赖专家定义约束来缩小搜索空间；深度强化学习虽有效但计算成本高、环境代价大且可解释性差。本文旨在探索更高效、更可解释且环境友好的替代方案。

Method: 将MNEP重新表述为非马尔可夫奖励决策过程（NMRDP），采用表格型强化学习（tabular RL）替代Deep RL；在奖励函数中融入社会公平性标准（效率和公平性）；在西安和 Amsterdam 真实路网数据上进行评估。

Result: 与Deep RL相比，训练总轮次平均减少18倍，总碳排放量平均减少12倍，同时性能与Deep RL保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格型RL方法在MNEP上是一种可复现、模块化、可解释且资源高效的解决方案，并有望推广到其他组合优化问题中。

Abstract: We tackle the Metro Network Expansion Problem (MNEP), a subset of the Transport Network Design Problem (TNDP), which focuses on expanding metro systems to satisfy travel demand. Traditional methods rely on exact and heuristic approaches that require expert-defined constraints to reduce the search space. Recently, deep reinforcement learning (Deep RL) has emerged due to its effectiveness in complex sequential decision-making processes-it remains, however, computationally expensive, environmentally costly, and requires additional engineering to interpret. We show that MNEP problems are small enough to not require Deep RL methods. Reformulating the MNEP as a Non-Markovian Rewards Decision Process (NMRDP), we use tabular RL to achieve similar performance with significantly fewer training episodes, additionally offering greater interpretability. Additionally, we incorporate social equity criteria into the reward functions, focusing on efficiency and fairness, highlighting the versatility of our method. Evaluated in real-world settings-Xi'an and Amsterdam-our method reduces total episodes by a factor of 18 and total carbon emissions by a factor of 12 on average, while remaining competitive with Deep RL. This approach offers a replicable, modular, interpretable, and resource-efficient solution with potential applications to other combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [156] [When Autoregressive Consistency Hurts Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04168)
*Bochen Lyu,Yiyang Jia,Xiaohao Cai,Zhanxing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型安全对齐的脆弱性根源于自回归一致性——模型倾向于维持当前回复轨迹的一致性。这种机制使得安全对齐主要集中在早期token上，导致对齐浅层化。基于此，作者提出随机插入攻击，通过在安全拒绝回复中插入有害片段，利用自回归一致性使模型转向有害输出。作为防御，提出对抗性安全对齐框架，通过最坏情况有害连续状态训练来增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐方法脆弱且浅层，微调主要改变前几个输出token的行为。作者希望从自回归一致性的角度给出这一现象的机理解释，并基于此设计更有效的攻击与防御方法。

Method: 1. 理论分析：从自回归一致性的学习动力学角度解释安全对齐集中于早期token的内在机制。2. 攻击方法：提出随机插入攻击，在安全拒绝回复中插入短有害片段，利用自回归一致性维持有害分支。3. 防御方法：提出对抗性安全对齐框架，基于最坏情况有害连续状态训练，实例化为随机最坏插入训练。

Result: 短有害片段即使出现在较长安全拒绝前缀之后，仍能引导模型转向有害生成。这表明自回归一致性是比单纯浅层对齐更广泛的失败机制。

Conclusion: 自回归一致性应作为安全对齐和攻击设计的核心考量因素。安全对齐需要在整个输出轨迹中打破有害的自回归一致性，而非仅关注早期token。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is fragile in part because it is often shallow: fine-tuning mainly reshapes the model's behavior near the first few output tokens. We argue that this phenomenon can be understood through autoregressive consistency, the tendency of next-token prediction to preserve and extend the current response trajectory consistently. By analyzing the learning dynamics of safety alignment, we show that autoregressive consistency can concentrate alignment updates on early tokens, offering a mechanistic explanation for shallow safety alignment. The same mechanism also predicts a broader class of attacks on LLMs: attacks that induce harmful continuation states at arbitrary positions in the output trajectory. As a concrete example, we introduce random insertion attack, which inserts a short harmful span into an otherwise safe refusal trajectory and exploits autoregressive consistency to sustain the resulting harmful branch, thereby bypassing safety alignment. Notably, a short harmful span can redirect the generation to be harmful even after a long refusal prefix, highlighting autoregressive consistency as a potential broader failure mechanism. This suggests that safety alignment should also break harmful autoregressive consistency throughout the output trajectory. We therefore propose adversarial safety alignment, an initial framework based on worst-case harmful continuation states, and instantiate it with random worst-insertion training. Overall, our results suggest that autoregressive consistency should be treated as a central consideration in both safety alignment and attack design.

</details>


### [157] [Low-rank Distributional Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04176)
*Jiayi Wang,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分布化矩阵补全问题，其中矩阵条目为概率分布而非标量。通过核均值嵌入表示分布，并提出分布值矩阵的Tucker秩概念，引入函数展开算子连接分布低秩结构与经典张量Tucker秩，据此提出新估计器，建立非渐近误差界，实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵补全处理标量值条目，但许多实际场景中矩阵条目本质上是概率分布（如不同用户在不同情境下的行为分布）。直接观测分布不可行，仅能获得有限样本。需要将矩阵补全推广到分布值场景，同时处理低秩结构和采样不确定性。

Method: 采用核均值嵌入将分布映射到再生核希尔伯特空间，定义分布值矩阵的Tucker秩。引入函数展开算子将无限维的分布低秩结构转化为有限维张量的经典Tucker秩，从而利用成熟张量分解工具进行估计。

Result: 建立了所提估计器的非渐近误差界，刻画统计性能。合成数据与真实数据实验表明方法有效。

Conclusion: 成功将矩阵补全推广到分布值设定，通过核嵌入与函数展开算子架起分布低秩结构与经典张量分解的桥梁，为处理分布型矩阵数据提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: We study a distributional generalization of the matrix completion problem in which each entry of the target matrix is a probability distribution rather than a scalar. In this setting, only a subset of matrix entries is observed, and even for observed entries, the underlying distributions are not directly accessible; instead, we observe finitely many samples drawn from them. To represent distributional entries, we employ kernel mean embeddings and introduce a notion of Tucker rank for distribution-valued matrices to capture their low-rank structure. The infinite-dimensional nature of kernel embeddings poses significant methodological challenges. To address this, we introduce functional unfolding operators that link the proposed distributional low-rank structure to the classical Tucker rank for finite-dimensional tensors. Based on this framework, we propose a novel estimator for distributional matrix completion. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds that characterize the statistical performance of the estimator. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [158] [KODA: Contrastive Representation Comparison and Alignment for Vision-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04180)
*Youqi Wu,Mohammad Jalali,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KODA框架，通过核方法对比分析CLIP与SigLIP等视觉-语言基础模型表征的结构性差异，能够发现一个表征中聚类紧密而另一表征中聚类松散的样本子集，并提供可解释的差异方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型（如CLIP、SigLIP）的性能评估多依赖下游任务指标，未能揭示其表征之间的结构性差异，缺乏系统性的对比分析工具。

Method: 提出Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis (KODA)框架：通过模态核组合构建统一多模态核，将差异发现建模为约束优化问题，在一个表征中搜索紧密结构的同时抑制参考表征中的结构；采用随机傅里叶特征等随机投影技术实现大规模数据集上的核近似。

Result: KODA能够识别视觉-语言表征中一致且可解释的差异结构，并提供用于表征对齐的样本子集。

Conclusion: KODA为对比分析视觉-语言模型表征结构差异提供了有效的核方法框架，兼具可解释性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models such as CLIP and SigLIP provide widely used representations for multimodal learning systems. While these models are typically compared through downstream performance, such evaluations often do not explain how their representations differ structurally. In this work, we study this problem through the task of Contrastive Embedding Clustering: identifying sample subsets that are weakly clustered under one representation but strongly clustered under another. We propose \emph{Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis (KODA)}, a kernel-based framework for contrastive representation comparison and alignment. KODA constructs unified multimodal kernels through modality-wise kernel composition and formulates discrepancy discovery as a constrained optimization problem that searches for coherent structures in one representation while suppressing coherence in a reference representation. This yields interpretable discrepancy directions associated with specific sample subsets and modality interactions. To scale KODA to large vision-language datasets, we develop randomized low-dimensional approximations of joint kernels using random projections, including Random Fourier Features for shift-invariant kernels. Empirically, KODA identifies consistent and interpretable discrepancy structures across vision-language representations and provides sample subsets for representation alignment. The code is available at https://github.com/yokiwuuu/KODA.

</details>


### [159] [Exact Unlearning in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04182)
*Thanh Nguyen-Tang,Raman Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化学习中的精确遗忘问题，设计了一种ρ-TV稳定的RL算法，使得在用户请求删除数据后，在线学习器的输出与从未接触过该用户数据的输出不可区分。该算法在表格型MDP中实现了遗憾界$\mathcal{O}(H^2 \sqrt{SAT} + H^3 S^2 A + {H^{2.5} S^2 A}/ρ)$，并证明了下界$\Omega(H\sqrt{\!SAT}\! +\! {SAH}/ρ)$，表明算法近乎极小化最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据隐私法规（如GDPR）的推行，用户有权要求删除其数据。在强化学习场景中，传统的"删除后重训"方法计算成本极高，因此需要设计一种高效且能保证精确遗忘（输出不可区分性）的RL算法。

Method: 定义了ρ-TV稳定性的精确遗忘框架，使算法输出在统计距离上与原结果不可区分；构建了针对表格型MDP的ρ-TV稳定RL算法，其期望计算成本仅为从头重训成本的$ρ\sqrt{\ln T}$比例。

Result: 算法遗憾界为$\mathcal{O}(H^2 \sqrt{SAT} + H^3 S^2 A + {H^{2.5} S^2 A}/ρ)$，下界为$\Omega(H\sqrt{SAT} + SAH/ρ)$，算法近乎极小化最优。

Conclusion: 首次在强化学习中系统性研究了精确遗忘问题，提出的算法在保证隐私合规（可删除用户数据）的同时，显著降低了计算开销，且理论上接近最优。

Abstract: We formulate the problem of \emph{exact unlearning} in reinforcement learning, where the goal is to design an efficient framework that enables the removal of any user's data upon deletion request, i.e., the online learner's output after unlearning is \emph{indistinguishable} from what would have been produced had the deleted user never interacted with the learner. For any $ρ>0$, we show that there exists a reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm that is $ρ$-TV-stable and supports an exact unlearning procedure whose expected computational cost is only a $ρ\sqrt{\ln T}$ fraction of the computational cost of retraining from scratch. We construct such a $ρ$-TV-stable RL algorithm for tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs), which achieves a regret bound of $\mathcal{O}(H^2 \sqrt{SAT} + H^3 S^2 A + {H^{2.5} S^2 A}/ρ)$, where $S, A, H$, and $T$ denote the number of states, the number of actions, the episode horizon, and the number of episodes, respectively. We also establish a lower bound of $Ω(H\sqrt{\!SAT}\! +\! {SAH}/ρ)$ for $ρ$-TV-stable RL algorithms, showing that our algorithm is nearly minimax optimal.

</details>


### [160] [Dual Advantage Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04188)
*Alexey Zemtsov,Maxim Bobrin,Alexander Nikulin,Dmitry V. Dylov,Fakhri Karray,Vladislav Kurenkov,Martin Takáč,Arip Asadulaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dual Advantage Fields (DAF)方法，通过双线性对偶价值模型提取局部优势信号，实现离线目标条件强化学习中的策略提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习需要同时具备长时域可达性估计和局部动作比较能力。现有对偶目标表征能捕捉全局目标可达性，但无法直接指定当前状态下应优先选择哪个动作。

Method: DAF学习一个动作效应模型，预测动作引起的折扣特征位移，并通过该位移与目标方向的对齐程度对动作评分。在双线性对偶参数化下，目标嵌入是值场关于状态表示的梯度。

Result: 在OGBench的 locomotion、manipulation 和 puzzle 任务中，DAF 提升了聚合 RLiable 指标，在局部正确动作与直接朝向最终目标动作不同的场景中表现强劲。

Conclusion: DAF在可实现情况下，该评分等于目标条件Bellman优势，具备标准的局部策略改进保证，有效弥合了全局可达性与局部动作选择之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning requires both long-horizon reachability estimates and local action comparisons. Dual goal representations provide value fields that capture global goal reachability, but they do not directly specify which action should be preferred at a given state. We propose Dual Advantage Fields, a policy-extraction method that turns a bilinear dual value model into a local advantage signal. Under bilinear dual parameterization, the goal embedding is the gradient of the value field with respect to the state representation. DAF learns an action-effect model that predicts the discounted feature displacement induced by an action and scores actions by the alignment between this displacement and the goal direction. In the realizable case, this score equals the goal-conditioned Bellman advantage, yielding a standard local policy-improvement guarantee. On OGBench locomotion, manipulation, and puzzle tasks, DAF improves aggregate RLiable metrics and performs strongly in settings where locally correct actions differ from direct movement toward the final goal.

</details>


### [161] [Metric-Aware Hybrid Forecasting for the CTF4Science Lorenz Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04191)
*Cen Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CTF4Science Lorenz挑战，构建了度量感知混合系统：合成预训练去噪器用于轨迹重建、Lorenz ODE拟合与轨迹投射用于前20步预测、基于合成Lorenz库的直方图尾部替换用于长期评估，在公开排行榜上取得83.83551分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现单一模型族无法在所有度量上取得最优表现，因此需要为不同度量家族分配不同的预测器。

Method: 构建度量感知混合系统：(1) 合成预训练去噪器用于全轨迹重建；(2) Lorenz ODE拟合与轨迹投射用于前20步预测；(3) 基于合成Lorenz库的直方图尾部替换用于长期评估。

Result: 代表性成熟提交得分83.83551，后续改进达到83.85529。

Conclusion: 通过为不同度量任务分配专门预测器的混合系统优于任何单一模型族，且中间系统版本具有更好的可复现性和可分析性。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the CTF4Science Lorenz challenge, a benchmark that mixes short-horizon forecasting, long-time distribution matching, and trajectory reconstruction across nine task pairs. The key discovery is that no single model family dominated all metrics. Instead, we built a metric-aware hybrid system that assigned a different predictor to each metric family: (1) synthetic-pretrained denoisers for full-trajectory reconstruction, (2) Lorenz ODE fitting and trajectory shooting for the first 20 forecast steps, and (3) histogram-tail substitution using synthetic Lorenz libraries for long-time evaluation. A representative mature submission from this system family scored 83.83551 on the public leaderboard, and a small follow-up stack of the same ideas reached 83.85529. We focus on the cleaner intermediate system because it captures the full method while remaining simple enough to reproduce and analyze, while the final submission can be understood as a conservative extension of the same backbone.

</details>


### [162] [Training-Free Lexical-Dense Fusion for Conversational-Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04194)
*Christian Lysenstøen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统复现并扩展了Nano-Memory中提出的"回合隔离检索"（基于最大查询-轮次相似度的晚交互评分）方法，提出了四项关键发现：(1) 将晚交互密集分数与BM25进行分数级融合（仅需一个留一对话法权重），在LoCoMo数据集上Hit@1提升8.8-17.2点；(2) 现成的Web搜索交叉编码器重排序反而降低性能；(3) 池化算子消融实验表明top-k晚交互等价于最大相似度，但朴素smooth-max对半数编码器失效；(4) 晚交互与基线差距随编码器规模增大而增大，但融合增益反而缩小。本文贡献是一个受控、可复现的无训练检索方案，而非提出新的检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多会话长对话历史中检索与当前查询相关的过去几个轮次，是长时对话记忆的检索瓶颈。已有工作Nano-Memory提出用最大查询-轮次相似度（晚交互/"回合隔离检索"）来评分对话会话，优于均值池化的对话嵌入。本文旨在复现该效果，并进一步探究一个无需训练、仅需CPU的检索阶段应如何围绕此方法进行增强。

Method: (1) 分数级融合：将晚交互密集分数与BM25分数在单一留一对话法权重下进行融合；(2) 重排序实验：使用现成的Web搜索交叉编码器对融合后的top-10结果进行重排序；(3) 池化算子消融：比较top-k晚交互、最大相似度、smooth-max（log-sum-exp）等不同池化策略；(4) 晚-早差距分析：分析六种不同编码器下晚交互与基线的差距及融合增益的变化趋势。

Result: (1) Fusion方法在LoCoMo上Hit@1达0.752/NDCG@5达0.829（e5-large-v2），比纯晚交互提升8.8-17.2点，比BM25提升11.2点（全部p<1e-4）；(2) 交叉编码器重排序反而使Hit@1下降6.9点；(3) top-k晚交互等价于最大相似度，但smooth-max对半数编码器效果崩溃；(4) 晚-早差距随编码器规模增大而扩大，但融合增益反而缩小；在LongMemEval-S（BM25饱和的词汇主导场景）上，融合增益不显著。类别分析表明：晚交互对多跳和时间类问题帮助最大，但在对抗性问题中不如BM25。

Conclusion: 本文提供一个受控、可复现的无训练检索方案，验证了分数级融合（晚交互+BM25）的强效性，并揭示了重排序失效、池化算子敏感性、编码器规模与融合增益的负相关等关键发现。作者明确指出本文贡献是检索阶段的系统性研究，而非完整的记忆架构或新的检索器。

Abstract: Retrieving the few past turns that answer a new query across long multi-session histories is the retrieval bottleneck behind long-term conversational memory (LoCoMo, LongMemEval). Recent concurrent work, Nano-Memory, shows that scoring a session by the maximum query-turn similarity (late interaction, "Turn Isolation Retrieval") beats mean-pooled session embeddings. We do not claim that effect; we replicate it and ask what a training-free, CPU-only retrieval stage should add around it. We report four findings. (1) Fuse: score-level fusion of the late-interaction dense score with BM25, under a single leave-one-conversation-out weight, adds +8.8 to +17.2 points of LoCoMo Hit@1 over late interaction alone across six encoders (all p<1e-4), reaching Hit@1 0.752 / NDCG@5 0.829 (e5-large-v2), +11.2 pp over BM25. (2) An off-the-shelf web-search cross-encoder reranker over the fused top-10 hurts here, degrading Hit@1 by 6.9 pp (one reranker, one configuration). (3) A pooling-operator ablation shows top-k late interaction matches max-similarity, but a naive smooth-max (log-sum-exp) collapses for half the encoders. (4) The late-minus-early gap is large for all six encoders and tends to be larger for larger ones, while the marginal fusion gain shrinks; on LongMemEval-S, a lexical regime where BM25 saturates, the net fusion gain over BM25 is small and not significant. A per-category analysis frames the gain as a division of labor: dense late interaction helps most on multi-hop and temporal questions but trails BM25 on adversarial ones. The contribution is a controlled, reproducible account of a strong training-free retrieval recipe, not the late-interaction retriever itself (Nano-Memory's). We make no claim to a complete memory architecture; this is a retrieval-stage study.

</details>


### [163] [A Geometric View of Counterfactual Behavior: Interaction of Boundary Proximity and Local Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04209)
*Ioanna Gemou,Matteo Gamba,Randall Balestriero,Ritambhara Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在预训练编码器+线性分类头的视觉、语言和多模态系统中，不同模型在反事实解释行为上的差异。结果表明，即使预测性能相似，模型在反事实可行性上存在显著差异；仅改变分类头即可大幅改变反事实行为而不影响准确性。这种差异由决策边界邻近度和局部数据支持共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注反事实解释的生成方法，但不同模型在反事实行为上的系统性差异及其与预测性能的关系尚未被充分探索。理解这一差异对于模型选择、鲁棒性和反事实方法的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 使用标准化的局部搜索探针，在多个预训练编码器和线性分类头上进行实验。通过固定表征空间、仅改变分类头来隔离反事实行为差异，并分析决策边界邻近度与局部数据支持量化特征与反事实结果的关系。

Result: 1) 预测性能相似的模型在反事实行为上存在显著差异；2) 固定表征仅改变分类头可大幅改变反事实结果而保持性能不变；3) 决策边界邻近度和局部数据支持的交互作用是解释这种差异的关键因素；4) 该发现可用于改进固定模型内的反事实搜索。

Conclusion: 反事实行为是独立于预测性能的额外维度，可在不改变准确率的情况下被调整，这对模型选择、鲁棒性评估及反事实解释方法的可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations seek small, semantically meaningful changes to an input that alter a model's prediction, and are widely used to interpret and audit machine learning systems. In modern vision, language, and multimodal systems, pretrained encoders map inputs to representation spaces, and downstream classifier heads impose decision boundaries within those spaces. As a result, the feasibility and distance of nearby counterfactuals depend on boundary placement relative to the data. Yet models with similar predictive performance can differ substantially in whether such changes are achievable and how far representations must move. This work examines this variation using a standardized local search probe across several pretrained encoders and linear classifier heads. Results show that despite similar predictive performance, models differ substantially in their counterfactual behavior. Under fixed representations, varying only the classifier head alters counterfactual outcomes while leaving predictive performance largely unchanged. This variation is explained by the interaction of decision-boundary proximity and local data support, which jointly determine whether prediction changes are both feasible and lie in regions supported by the data, and can also improve counterfactual search within fixed models. Together, these findings identify counterfactual behavior as a distinct dimension beyond predictive performance and show that it can be altered without changing accuracy, with implications for model selection, robustness, and the reliability of counterfactual methods.

</details>


### [164] [Edge of Stability Selectively Shapes Learning Across the Data Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04212)
*Shauna Kwag,Anakha Ganesh,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现边缘稳定性（EoS）不仅是优化的全局属性，还具有选择性：它会重新分配学习资源，使某些数据组受益而抑制其他组。通过分支干预实验，证明这种权衡依赖于两个条件：该组的聚合梯度与Hessian主特征向量对齐，且梯度幅度不随时间消失。在交叉熵损失下，梯度饱和使自信分类的组失去优势，转向输出异常值组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将EoS视为全局优化现象，但对其在不同数据子集上的差异性影响尚不清楚。作者试图揭示EoS是否以及如何在不同训练组之间产生选择性学习分配效应。

Method: 采用分支干预方法，从同一训练状态进入或退出EoS regime，因果性地证明这种权衡。通过受控扰动（保持距离但随机化方向）破坏对齐机制，并分析交叉熵损失下的梯度饱和效应。

Result: 1) EoS具有选择性，对不同数据组产生不同学习影响。2) 组受益EoS的两个必要条件：聚合梯度与Hessian主特征向量对齐，以及梯度幅度持续非零。3) 交叉熵损失下，梯度饱和使自信分类组失去优势，输出异常值组获得优势。

Conclusion: EoS不仅是稳定性边界，更是跨数据分布分配学习资源的关键机制。这一发现揭示了优化动力学中的学习分配规律，对理解深度学习训练过程具有重要意义。

Abstract: Existing analyses of the edge of stability (EoS) treat it as a global property of optimization. We show that it is also selective: the stability constraint redistributes learning across subsets of the training distribution, amplifying progress on some groups while suppressing progress on others. Using a branching intervention that enters or exits the EoS regime from the same training state, we causally demonstrate this trade-off and identify two necessary conditions for a group to benefit. First, its aggregate gradient must align with the top Hessian eigenvector. We isolate this mechanism with a controlled perturbation that preserves distance but randomizes direction, destroying alignment and eliminating the advantage. Second, the group must sustain non-vanishing gradient magnitude over time. Under cross-entropy loss, gradient saturation decouples confidently classified groups, shifting the advantage to output-outliers, whose gradients persist. Together, these results show that EoS functions not only as a stability boundary, but as a mechanism governing the allocation of learning across the data distribution.

</details>


### [165] [Recover-LoRA for Aggressive Quantization: Reclaiming Accuracy in 2-Bit Language Models via Low-Rank Adaptation with Knowledge Distillation on Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04238)
*Devleena Das,Rajeev Patwari,Elliott Delaye,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Recover-LoRA方法，通过选择性混合精度量化（仅将MLP的gate和up投影层量化为2-bit，其余层保持高精度）结合轻量级无数据精度恢复技术，在Qwen3-4B等模型上实现7.5-23.3%的TPS提升，并在9/12基准测试中恢复80-95%的精度损失，仅需10k合成样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端权重量化（2-bit）虽可显著提升LLM推理吞吐和内存效率（尤其适用于边缘设备），但会导致严重精度下降。现有方法缺乏针对超低位宽量化的轻量级、无数据精度恢复方案。

Method: 1) 提出W4/W2-GateUp混合精度策略：仅将MLP模块的gate和up投影层量化为2-bit（W2），其余线性层保持4-bit（W4）；2) 应用Recover-LoRA在量化层上训练低秩适配器，通过合成数据上的logit蒸馏恢复精度，无需真实标注数据。

Result: 在4B-20B三个模型家族和两种硬件平台上，W4/W2-GateUp相比均匀W4量化实现7.5-23.3%的TPS提升。Qwen3-4B案例中，Recover-LoRA在12个基准的9个上恢复80-95%精度，仅用10k合成样本；合成数据与标注数据效果相当，且恢复能力泛化到分布外任务。

Conclusion: Recover-LoRA是一种实用的部署后精度恢复工具，能够有效补偿激进权重量化带来的精度损失，兼具数据无关、轻量级和强泛化性的优势。

Abstract: Aggressive weight quantization to 2-bit precision offers substantial throughput and memory gains for large language model (LLM) inference, but typically incurs severe accuracy degradation. These gains are particularly relevant for edge and on-device deployment, where memory capacity and bandwidth are primary constraints. In this work, we extend Recover-LoRA -- a lightweight, data-free accuracy recovery method originally developed for general model weight corruption -- to the setting of ultra-low-bit quantization. We propose a selective mixed-precision strategy in which only gate and up projection layers of the MLP are quantized to 2-bit (W2), while all other linear layers remain at higher precision, yielding a mixed-precision GateUp configuration. We demonstrate via roofline analysis across three model families (4B--20B) and two hardware platforms that a W4/W2-GateUp deployment (4-bit base with 2-bit gate/up) delivers 7.5--23.3\% TPS improvement over uniform W4 depending on model and context length, while confining quantization error to a predictable subset of layers. We then apply Recover-LoRA -- training low-rank adapters on the quantized layers via logit distillation with synthetic data -- to recover accuracy lost from 2-bit quantization of the gate and up layers. In a case study on Qwen3-4B, Recover-LoRA achieves 80--95\% accuracy recovery on 9 of 12 benchmarks, using only 10k synthetic training samples and no labeled data. We further demonstrate that synthetic data performs comparably to curated labeled data for distillation-based recovery, and that recovery generalizes to out-of-distribution evaluation tasks. Our results present Recover-LoRA as a practical post-quantization accuracy recovery tool for aggressive weight compression in deployment settings.

</details>


### [166] [RL Excursions during Pre-Training: Re-examining Policy Optimization for LLM training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04272)
*Rachit Bansal,Clara Mohri,Tian Qin,David Alvarez-Melis,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了“RL仅在预训练和SFT之后使用”的常规做法，通过在中间检查点直接应用RL，发现RL在早期就有效，且平行合并RL与SFT目标的方法在保持通用能力的同时超越所有其他训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战LLM训练中RL仅在SFT之后使用的标准流程，探索RL能否更早、更有效地融入训练过程。

Method: 从零开始训练LLM，在中间预训练检查点上分别应用RL、SFT、SFT→RL；通过平行平均合并RL和SFT目标；实验涵盖不同难度问题和数据组成。

Result: RL在训练早期即有效，常匹配完整SFT→RL流程；针对性预训练数据组成比模型规模更影响RL效果；直接对基础检查点应用RL会扩展模型分布（锐化效应仅在RL跟随SFT时出现）；SFT会降低通用能力而RL几乎不改变；平行合并RL和SFT目标在所有指标上表现最优。

Conclusion: LLM训练可能受益于RL的更广泛使用，平行合并RL与SFT目标是一种有前景的训练范式。

Abstract: The standard LLM training pipeline applies reinforcement learning (RL) only after pre-training and supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We question this status quo by training a LLM from scratch and applying RL, SFT, and SFT followed by RL directly to intermediate pre-training checkpoints. We find that RL is effective very early, and often matches the full SFT$\to$RL pipeline early as well. Through experiments on harder problems, we find that targeted pre-training data composition is a strong lever for RL effectiveness, even more so than model scale. Beyond reasoning accuracy, applying RL directly to base checkpoints expands the model's distribution; the sharpening effect reported in recent work arises only when RL follows SFT. The general capabilities of the model remain essentially unchanged by RL, while they degrade following SFT. Finally, we merge RL and SFT objectives by parallel averaging, which outperforms across all other training methods discussed, across metrics, while preserving general capabilities. Together, these results suggest that LLM training might benefit from an expanded use of RL.

</details>


### [167] [From Ticks to Flows: Dynamics of Neural Reinforcement Learning in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04275)
*Saket Tiwari,Tejas Kotwal,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于连续环境中深度强化学习的新理论框架，通过将问题建模为连续时间随机过程，推导了过参数化神经网络演员-评论员算法在无限宽极限下的状态分布演化方程，并进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习理论分析多针对离散环境或有限宽网络，缺乏对连续环境中过参数化神经网络演员-评论员算法的严格理论刻画，尤其是在状态分布随梯度步长的演化方面。

Method: 将连续环境中的深度强化学习建模为连续时间随机过程；引入双时间尺度过程（环境时间和梯度时间）；在两层神经网络的无限宽极限下，利用随机微分方程理论推导状态分布和累积折扣回报估计量的演化方程。

Result: 首次在连续强化学习中推导出描述每个梯度步长下状态分布无穷小变化的方程；建立了过参数化神经演员-评论员算法的非参数化形式；通过玩具连续控制任务进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究过参数化神经演员-评论员算法提供了一个新颖的非参数化理论框架，为连续环境深度强化学习的理论分析开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework for deep reinforcement learning (RL) in continuous environments by modeling the problem as a continuous-time stochastic process, drawing on insights from stochastic control. Building on previous work, we introduce a viable model of actor-critic algorithm that incorporates both exploration and stochastic transitions. For single-hidden-layer neural networks, we show that the state of the environment can be formulated as a two time scale process: the environment time and the gradient time. Within this formulation, we characterize how the time-dependent random variables that represent the environment's state and estimate of the cumulative discounted return evolve over gradient steps in the infinite width limit of two-layer networks. Using the theory of stochastic differential equations, we derive, for the first time in continuous RL, an equation describing the infinitesimal change in the state distribution at each gradient step, under a vanishingly small learning rate. Overall, our work provides a novel nonparametric formulation for studying overparametrized neural actor-critic algorithms. We empirically corroborate our theoretical result using a toy continuous control task.

</details>


### [168] [Derivative Informed Learning of Exchange-Correlation Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04279)
*Eike S. Eberhard,Luca A. Thiede,Abdul Aldossary,Andreas Burger,Nicholas Gao,Vignesh Bhethanabotla,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Derivative Informed XC-Loss (DI-Loss)，通过监督能量对密度矩阵的Grassmannian上的一阶和二阶导数，训练O(N^3)标度的ML-XC泛函来复现B3LYP/def2-SVP目标。相比仅监督能量和密度，DI-Loss使总能量MAE平均降低66%，平均场能量误差从1.2降至0.8 mEh，且蒸馏泛函可减少混合泛函SCF迭代达50%，在TDDFT中激发态MAE降低19-35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习交换关联泛函虽然旨在通过学习参考数据替代传统泛函，但仍未能持续超越O(N^4)标度的混合泛函性能。其核心问题在于仅匹配自洽不动点，缺乏对泛函导数信息的有效约束，导致泛函响应行为与目标不一致。

Method: 提出DI-Loss，在Grassmannian流形上监督可容许密度矩阵的能量一阶和二阶导数；将ML-XC泛函的局部一阶和二阶响应与目标泛函对齐；结合能量和密度监督，在四种架构上训练O(N^3)标度的ML-XC泛函复现B3LYP/def2-SVP。

Result: 四种架构上总能量MAE平均降低66%；平均场能量误差从1.2降至0.8 mEh；蒸馏泛函可减少混合泛函SCF迭代达50%；XCdiff在TDDFT中激发态MAE降低19-35%。偶极和L2密度误差未一致改善。

Conclusion: DI-Loss通过Hessian监督有效提升了ML-XC泛函的精度和下游任务性能，验证了导数信息约束在密度泛函蒸馏中的关键作用，为开发更高效的ML-XC泛函提供了新范式。

Abstract: Machine-learned (ML) exchange-correlation (XC) functionals aim to replace human-designed density functional approximations by learning directly from reference data, but they still do not consistently outperform traditional $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$-scaling hybrid functionals. We study a hybrid-distillation setting in which $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$-scaling ML-XC functionals are trained to reproduce B3LYP/def2-SVP targets. We introduce Derivative Informed XC-Loss (DI-Loss), a loss that incorporates additional information from the reference hybrid functional by supervising first and second derivatives of the energy on the Grassmannian of admissible density matrices. Rather than only matching the self-consistent fixed point, DI-Loss aligns the local first- and second-order response of the learned functional with that of the target functional. Across four evaluated architectures, DI-Loss consistently improves the main energy metrics. Averaged uniformly across architectures, the total-energy MAE decreases by 66% relative to energy and density supervision alone. The density-sensitive mean-field energy metric $E_ρ$ improves from $1.2$ to $0.8$ mEh on average, while dipole and $\mathcal{L}_2$ density errors do not improve uniformly. We further show that densities from the distilled functionals reduce hybrid-functional SCF iterations by up to 50%. In downstream TDDFT calculations, Hessian supervision improves excited-state predictions, with XCdiff reducing the mean excitation-energy MAE by 19 - 35%.

</details>


### [169] [The Loss Is Not Enough: Sampling Conditions and Inductive Bias in Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04280)
*Justinas Zaliaduonis,Patrick Putzky,Till Richter,Sergios Gatidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了度量论框架，形式化了对比学习中正对采样的“多样性条件”，证明该条件是等距潜在空间恢复的必要条件；标准von Mises-Fisher全支撑分布满足该条件，而受限条件分布可能导致非正交映射取得更低的渐近对比损失；提出支撑校正的InfoNCE变体可实现正交恢复但不唯一；实验验证了可识别性预测并揭示了采样多样性与编码器归纳偏置的交互关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习已成为自监督表征学习的主流范式，但其恢复有意义的潜在空间几何结构的条件尚未完全明确。现有理论分析多假设采样分布满足特定性质，但缺乏对必要条件的严格形式化刻画，亟需建立更系统的理论框架来理解对比学习的可识别性条件。

Method: 1）建立度量论框架，形式化定义“多样性条件”（对正对采样支撑集的要求）；2）理论分析标准von Mises-Fisher全支撑设定下全局对比损失最小化器可恢复至正交变换；3）发现受限条件分布下非正交映射可取得更低渐近对比损失；4）提出支撑校正的InfoNCE变体作为理论修正方案；5）通过合成基准和CIFAR-10实验验证理论预测。

Result: 1）多样性条件是等距潜在恢复的必要条件；2）全支撑设定下正交恢复成立，受限分布下非正交映射更优；3）支撑校正InfoNCE可使正交恢复可实现但不唯一；4）实验验证了可识别性预测；5）采样多样性有限时，编码器归纳偏置的重要性增加。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了对比学习中采样机制与编码器归纳偏置的交互作用：采样多样性决定了损失景观中正交恢复的可行性，而编码器偏置在多样性不足时起关键补充作用。支撑校正方法虽能恢复正交性但无法唯一确定，表明对比学习的可识别性本质上受采样分布约束。

Abstract: Contrastive learning has become a leading paradigm for self-supervised representation learning, yet the conditions under which it recovers meaningful latent geometry remain incompletely understood. We develop a measure-theoretic framework formalizing the diversity condition, a support requirement on positive-pair sampling that is necessary for isometric latent recovery. We show that the standard full-support von Mises-Fisher setting implies the satisfaction of the diversity condition and as a consequence global contrastive loss minimizers recover latent geometry up to orthogonal transformation, while restricted conditionals can make non-orthogonal maps attain strictly lower asymptotic contrastive loss. We introduce a support-corrected Information Noise Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) variant as a theoretical fix: this correction makes orthogonal latent space recovery achievable but does not uniquely select it. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks validate the identifiability predictions, and CIFAR-10 experiments are consistent with the qualitative prediction that architectural inductive bias becomes more important when sampling diversity is limited. Together, our results clarify how sampling mechanisms and encoder inductive bias interact in contrastive representation learning.

</details>


### [170] [Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models Learn Interpretable and Specialized Experts for Personalized Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04284)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Ji-Ung Lee,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种稀疏混合专家（MoE）奖励模型，通过稀疏路由和专家多样性训练，从二元偏好数据中学习可解释、解耦的偏好组件，提升LLM个性化对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法假设通用奖励函数，忽略人类偏好的多样性和异质性；多偏好组件方法虽能从不需额外标注的二元数据中学习，但学到的组件缺乏连贯性和解耦性，限制了解释性和个性化效果。

Method: 提出稀疏混合专家（MoE）奖励模型，在二元偏好数据训练过程中引入稀疏路由机制并鼓励专家多样性，使不同专家学习不同偏好模式。

Result: 在受控实验和真实场景实验中，稀疏MoE模型学到的路由模式具有可解释性，专家具有专门化功能；测试时个性化表现提升；适应后的专家权重变化为分析模型如何适应个性化偏好提供了定性分析视角。

Conclusion: 稀疏MoE奖励模型能有效从二元偏好数据中学习可解释且解耦的偏好组件，在无需额外标注成本的前提下提升个性化对齐效果。

Abstract: Preference modeling plays a central role in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), enabling large language models (LLMs) to align with human values. However, most existing approaches assume a universal reward function, neglecting the diversity and heterogeneity of human preferences. To address this limitation without additional annotation costs, recent work has proposed learning multiple preference components from binary data and combining them to model individual preferences. Nevertheless, these components often fail to capture coherent and disentangled patterns, limiting their interpretability and effectiveness for personalization. In this work, we propose a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward model that encourages sparse routing and expert diversity during training on binary preference data. Across controlled and real-world experiments, sparse MoE learns interpretable routing patterns and specialized experts. It also improves test-time personalization, and post-adaptation shifts in expert weights provide a qualitative lens for analyzing how the model adapts to personalized preferences.

</details>


### [171] [Scaling Novel Graph Generation via Lightweight Structure-Guided Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04287)
*Alessio Barboni,Massimiliano Lupo Pasini,Bishal Lakha,Edoardo Serra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级自回归图生成框架，通过结构引导拓扑排序将图序列化为规则边序列，实现近对数线性生成速度；结合探索增强与迭代细化的两阶段训练策略，在分子和非分子基准上提升了新颖性，同时保持高有效性和唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成模型存在扩展性差、新颖性不足的问题：扩散模型需昂贵的全邻接操作和长去噪链；自回归和混合模型至少具有二次复杂度；且这些模型常模仿训练图而非泛化生成新图。

Method: (1) 结构引导拓扑排序：将图序列化为规则的边序列，实现近 O(n log n) 的生成复杂度；(2) 两阶段训练策略：结合探索导向的数据增强与迭代细化，减少过拟合并促进可控新颖性；(3) 支持 LSTM 和 Mamba 风格因果序列主干，利用大内存加速器突破典型 GPU 限制进行长图序列实验。

Result: 在分子和非分子基准实验中，该方法在提升新颖性的同时，保持高有效性和唯一性。

Conclusion: 该轻量级自回归框架有效解决了图生成的可扩展性和新颖性瓶颈，为实际应用（如分子发现、电路设计、网络安全等）提供了更实用的生成方案。

Abstract: Generating realistic and diverse graphs is a key problem in machine learning, with applications in molecular discovery, circuit design, cybersecurity, and beyond. However, current graph generative models remain limited by scalability and novelty. Diffusion-based methods often require costly full-adjacency operations and long denoising chains, while many autoregressive and hybrid models have at least quadratic complexity. In addition, these models often imitate training graphs rather than generalize beyond them.
  We propose a lightweight autoregressive framework to address these issues. It uses a structure-guided topological ordering to serialize graphs into regular edge sequences, enabling near log-linear generation, and a two-phase training strategy that combines exploration-oriented augmentation with iterative refinement to reduce overfitting and promote controlled novelty.
  Experiments on molecular and non-molecular benchmarks show that our approach improves novelty while preserving high validity and uniqueness. The framework also supports both LSTM and Mamba-style causal sequence backbones, with large-memory accelerators enabling longer graph-sequence experiments beyond typical GPU limits.

</details>


### [172] [Offline-to-Online Learning in Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04305)
*Kushagra Chandak,Toshinori Kitamura,Xiaoqi Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机线性赌博机中结合离线数据集的在线学习问题，提出一种平衡离线与在线数据利用的算法，在前期依赖离线数据、后期增加探索，并证明了其相对于纯在线和纯离线方法均具有竞争力的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线的权衡在结构化环境中仍然理解不足，而该问题在实际中频繁出现，因此需要提出有效方法来平衡两者。

Method: 提出一种线性赌博机算法，在早期轮次依赖离线数据，随着时间推移逐渐增加在线探索，从而动态平衡离线数据利用与在线探索。

Result: 证明该方法在在线交互次数上实现亚线性遗憾（相对于最优动作），同时相对于离线参考的遗憾随离线样本量增加而降低；实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时与纯在线和纯离线解决方案竞争，有效处理离线到在线的权衡问题。

Abstract: We study online learning with an additional offline dataset in the stochastic linear bandit setting. Although this problem arises frequently in practice, the offline-to-online tradeoff remains poorly understood in structured environments. We propose a linear bandit algorithm that balances this tradeoff: it relies on offline data during early rounds, and increasingly favors exploration as the horizon grows. We establish regret bounds showing that our method is simultaneously competitive with both purely online and purely offline solutions. In particular, it achieves sublinear regret relative to the optimal action in the number of online interactions, while its regret relative to an offline reference decreases as the number of offline samples grows. Empirical results further demonstrate its effectiveness across various problem parameters.

</details>


### [173] [Folded Transport MCMC: Certifiable Quotient Posterior Computation for Symmetric Bayesian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04307)
*Jun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Folded Transport MCMC (FolT-MCMC)方法，通过在对称群的基本域上构建独立采样器直接对商后验进行推理，解决了有限对称性贝叶斯模型中标签置换导致的冗余多模态问题。该方法对称化学习到的归一化流形成商提议分布，并证明了LCNF振荡认证框架可迁移至商度量空间。在多种实验设置下，折叠后的认证改进比达到2倍至145倍，且在实际建筑加速度计数据中，非折叠方法认证无效而FolT-MCMC给出非空认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限对称性贝叶斯模型（如混合模型中的可交换分量）的后验分布在标签置换群作用下具有不变性，导致冗余多模态性，严重降低MCMC收敛诊断的有效性。现有方法难以直接对商后验进行高效推理和认证。

Method: 提出FolT-MCMC方法，在对称群的基本域上构建独立采样器来直接对商后验进行推理。通过对称化学习到的归一化流（normalising flow）来形成商提议分布。理论部分证明了LCNF振荡认证框架可迁移至商度量空间，并给出了稳定子校正的球质量边界和改进的覆盖半径。

Result: 在高斯混合模型(d=2-20)、标签切换目标(最多24个等价模式)和标准贝叶斯三分量混合后验上，折叠认证改进比范围为2倍至145倍，折叠认证几乎与维度无关。在超高层建筑台风实测加速度数据上，非折叠认证无效而FolT-MCMC给出非空认证。

Conclusion: FolT-MCMC能有效处理对称性导致的冗余多模态问题，显著提升MCMC认证质量，且在实际工程数据中展现出非折叠方法无法实现的认证能力。

Abstract: Bayesian models with finite symmetry - mixture models with exchangeable components, structural identification with closely-spaced modes - define posteriors that are invariant under a group of label permutations, creating redundant multimodality that degrades MCMC convergence diagnostics. We introduce Folded Transport MCMC (FolT-MCMC), which performs inference directly on the quotient posterior by constructing an independence sampler on the fundamental domain of the symmetry group. The quotient proposal is formed by symmetrising a learned normalising flow over the group orbits. We prove that the LCNF oscillation-based certification framework transfers to the quotient metric with a stabiliser-corrected ball-mass bound and improved covering radius, and that the quantile-core certified lower bound improves whenever the unfolded flow exhibits cross-mode proposal deficiency. On Gaussian mixtures (d = 2 - 20), label-switching targets (up to 24 equivalent modes), and a standard Bayesian three-component mixture posterior, the quantile-core certified improvement ratio ranges from 2x to 145x, with the folded certificate empirically nearly dimension-free. On real accelerometer data from a supertall building during Typhoon Mangkhut, FolT-MCMC yields a non-vacuous quantile-core certificate where the unfolded certificate is vacuous.

</details>


### [174] [Latent Anchor-Driven Test Generation for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04310)
*Bin Duan,Matthew B. Dwyer,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latte，一种基于VQ-VAE潜空间的黑盒DNN测试框架，通过在潜空间中进行种子中心化的单步变异，生成语义接近、多样且能揭示故障的测试用例，在多个数据集和模型上相比现有方法提升了故障暴露能力和行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN测试方法在输入空间或潜空间进行探索，但存在探索可控性、故障多样性和语义漂移之间的权衡，缺乏同时兼顾三者优势的方法。

Method: 使用预训练VQ-VAE将输入种子编码到潜空间，以种子为中心沿替代类锚点定义的方向进行单步潜变异，再经量化与解码映射回输入空间，从而在潜流形上探索种子局部邻域。

Result: 在5个数据集和10个DNN模型上，单模型和多模型测试场景下，Latte在相同预算内生成更多且更多样化的触发预测差异的测试用例，同时保持较低的语义漂移。

Conclusion: Latte通过在潜空间中进行种子邻域探索，有效平衡了可控性、故障多样性与语义保持，优于现有输入空间和潜空间方法。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) are increasingly being deployed in security-critical and safety-sensitive applications, which makes rigorous testing essential to identify and mitigate model weaknesses. Existing DNN testing approaches explore either the input space or a learned latent space. While latent-space generation can better maintain plausibility than direct input-space mutation, current methods still face a trade-off among exploration controllability, failure diversity, and seed-relative semantic drift. To overcome these limitations, we propose Latte, a black-box testing framework that generates semantically proximate, diverse, and fault-revealing test cases by leveraging the latent space. Specifically, Latte encodes each input seed with a pre-trained VQ-VAE and performs a seed-centered, one-step latent mutation along directions defined by anchors sampled from alternative classes, followed by quantization and decoding back to the input space. This explores local neighborhoods around each seed within the learned latent manifold, resulting in a larger number and broader diversity of oracle-triggering prediction discrepancies under the same budget. We evaluated Latte on 5 datasets and 10 DNN models in single-model and multi-model testing scenarios. Across the evaluated datasets and models, Latte improves fault exposure and behavioral diversity under matched testing budgets. Under the single-model setting, it also maintains low seed-relative semantic drift with respect to the source seeds.

</details>


### [175] [Testing Neural Networks via Bayesian-Guided Exploration of Decision Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04314)
*Bin Duan,Meiru Che,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BayesWarp测试框架，通过可解释显著性技术识别决策关键区域进行变异，并利用不确定性感知的贝叶斯优化自适应引导测试，在保持数据分布和语义邻近性的同时高效发现多样化模型故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络的测试方法（黑盒或白盒）主要采用全局变异或覆盖引导策略，难以在保持与原始数据分布和语义邻近性的同时高效发现多样化的模型故障。

Method: 1) 通过可解释显著性技术识别决策关键输入区域；2) 对这些关键区域进行变异；3) 使用不确定性感知的贝叶斯优化自适应引导测试过程。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和ImageNet上对六个神经网络模型的评估表明，BayesWarp在固定变异预算下提高了故障发现率、故障多样性、测试用例质量和关键神经元覆盖率。

Conclusion: BayesWarp提升了测试有效性，且使用生成的故障用例进行微调可改善模型性能。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, testing is essential to evaluate and improve their reliability. Existing testing methods, whether black-box or white-box, primarily use global mutation or coverage-guided strategies, both of which struggle to efficiently uncover diverse model failures while remaining proximate to the original data distribution and semantics. We propose BayesWarp, a testing framework that addresses this limitation by mutating decision-critical input regions identified via interpretable saliency techniques and adaptively guiding the testing process using an uncertainty-aware Bayesian Optimization strategy, enabling the discovery of diverse failures while preserving distributional and semantic proximity to the original data. Evaluation on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ImageNet across six neural network models shows that BayesWarp improves failure discovery, failure diversity, test case quality, and critical neuron coverage under a fixed mutation budget. These results demonstrate that BayesWarp improves testing effectiveness. Moreover, fine-tuning with the generated failure cases leads to improvements in model performance.

</details>


### [176] [OpenRFM: Dissecting Relational In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04320)
*Zhikai Chen,Junyu Yin,Jialiang Gu,Siheng Xiong,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Keren Zhou,Kai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了关系型基础模型（RFM）中开放模型与商业模型之间的性能差距来源，从模型和数据两个角度揭示了关系Transformer（RT）在关系级上下文学习中的标签稀缺问题以及预训练分布差异导致的懒惰学习与特征学习的不同机制。基于此提出OpenRFM，包含双阶段上下文学习架构和同质性感知的合成+持续真实数据预训练混合策略，在多个评估任务上提升约30%性能并超越商业模型KumoRFMv1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放RFM与商业RFM之间存在显著的性能差距，但这一差距的根源尚未被系统地理解。

Method: （1）从模型侧：通过核回归视角证明RT执行关系级ICL，在标签-单元覆盖稀疏时因回归欠定而失效；（2）从数据侧：通过消融预训练源发现纯合成预训练与分布内预训练将相同架构驱动至不同学习机制（懒惰vs.特征学习），缺失的关键要素是标签生成过程中支持可识别的关系隐变量。基于此提出OpenRFM：双阶段ICL架构（关系骨干+批级ICL层）以缓解关系级标签稀缺，以及同质性感知的合成+持续真实数据预训练混合+原型正则化。

Result: OpenRFM在RT骨干上平均任务性能提升约30%，并超越商业模型KumoRFMv1。

Conclusion: 揭示了开放RFM落后的根源在于关系级ICL的标签稀疏性问题和预训练数据分布导致的特征学习机制差异，提出的OpenRFM有效弥合了与商业模型的差距。

Abstract: Relational Foundation Models (RFMs) promise a single pre-trained predictor that, given any relational database, returns predictions in one forward pass via relational in-context learning (ICL). Yet a substantial gap separates open RFMs from their commercial counterparts, and the origin of this gap has not been systematically understood. We dissect a representative framework, the Relational Transformer (RT), from two perspectives. Model side: we show that RT performs relation-level ICL, and a kernel regression view shows it fails when sparse label-cell coverage yields an underdetermined regression. Data side: we ablate RT's pre-training source and find that existing synthetic-only pre-training and in-distribution pre-training drive the same architecture into different regimes, lazy vs. feature-learning. Probing this gap reveals that the missing ingredient is a support-identifiable relational latent in the label-generation process. These two diagnoses translate into (1) a dual-stage ICL architecture that combines the relational backbone with a batch-level ICL layer lifted from a pre-trained tabular foundation model to overcome relation-level label scarcity, and (2) a homophily-aware synthetic plus continual real-data pre-training mixture, augmented with a prototype-based regularization. These choices define OpenRFM, a simple yet effective RFM that improves average task performance by approximately 30% over the RT backbone and surpasses the commercial model KumoRFMv1 on a large set of evaluation tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Neural Galerkin Normalizing Flows for Bayesian Inference of Diffusions with Inaccessible Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04324)
*Riccardo Saporiti,Fabio Nobile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Normalizing Flow的新架构，在神经Galerkin框架下求解带有Dirac初始条件的Fokker-Planck方程，学习扩散过程在两次观测时间之间的转移密度函数，从而实现高效的贝叶斯推断，特别适用于扩散矩阵在边界区域消失的随机波动率模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型贝叶斯推断的主要挑战在于观测数据间的转移密度函数缺乏解析表达式，难以获得似然函数。

Method: 提出新的Normalizing Flow架构，在神经Galerkin框架下求解带有Dirac质量初始条件的Fokker-Planck方程，学习扩散过程在两观测时间之间的转移密度函数。

Result: 获得的转移密度乘积近似似然函数，实现通过MCMC进行低成本后验采样。

Conclusion: 离线训练后，推断效率显著提升，无需实时求解FP方程或依赖其他无似然方法。

Abstract: One of the primary challenges in Bayesian inference on the parameters of a diffusion model from discrete observations is the unavailability of an analytical expression for the transition density function between consecutive observation times, which is needed to derive the likelihood function.
  Extending previous studies that solve Fokker-Planck (FP) type partial differential equations with Normalizing Flows, we propose a new Normalizing Flow architecture to learn the transition density function of the diffusion process between two observation times. We do so by solving in a Neural Galerkin framework the associated FP equation with a Dirac mass as initial condition, over a specified training distribution of the initial datum and the coefficients of the diffusion. We specifically focus on processes whose diffusion matrix vanishes in certain inaccessible boundary regions, such as Stochastic Volatility models that satisfy a Feller condition.
  The product of the obtained transition densities evaluated along the observed trajectory approximates the likelihood function, thereby enabling cheap posterior sampling via Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). After the offline training phase, inference becomes significantly more efficient, as it avoids the need to solve the FP equation in real time for each parameter proposed by the MCMC sampler or to rely on other likelihood-free methods for Bayesian inference that involve repeated simulation of diffusion bridges.

</details>


### [178] [Measuring What Matters: Synthetic Benchmarks for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04326)
*Julian Skirzynski,Harry Cheon,Shreyas Kadekodi,Meredith Stewart,Berk Ustun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为概念瓶颈模型开发了合成基准测试，用于评估其在决策支持和自动化两类主要应用场景中的表现，能够控制数据模态、概念选择、注释质量和完整性等影响性能的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集缺乏概念标签，限制了研究者对概念瓶颈模型的适用问题、性能驱动因素、失败原因及算法效果的深入理解。

Method: 构建可生成标注数据集的合成基准，控制数据模态、概念选择、注释质量和完整性等属性，并用于评估代表性概念瓶颈模型。

Result: 展示了合成基准能够诊断模型失败模式并指导后续测试。

Conclusion: 合成基准为概念瓶颈模型的系统评估提供了有效工具，有助于理解模型在不同条件下的行为表现。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models predict outcomes from high-level concepts detected in inputs. Although concepts provide a simple way to reap benefits from interpretability, very few datasets include concept labels. This limits researchers' ability to determine which problems are suitable for these models, isolate the factors that drive their performance or lead to failures, or uncover which algorithms perform well. In this paper, we develop synthetic benchmarks for concept-bottleneck models, focusing on their two main use cases: decision support, in which models assist humans in making better decisions, and automation, in which models handle routine tasks without supervision. Our benchmarks can generate labeled datasets while controlling for properties that affect performance, including data modality, concept choice, annotation quality, and completeness. We demonstrate how the benchmarks can be used to evaluate representative classes of concept bottleneck models. Our demonstrations show how the benchmarks can diagnose failure modes and guide follow-up testing.

</details>


### [179] [Policy Gradient for Continuous-Time Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04335)
*Tanya Veeravalli,David M. Bossens,Atsushi Nitanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的策略梯度算法，推导了路径依赖和伴随公式的策略梯度与对抗梯度，提出了双循环优化器（线性收敛、样本复杂度$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/ε^2)$）和均场优化器（$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/K)$收敛率、$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^2/ε)$样本复杂度），并在神经ODE动力学上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RMDP考虑离散时间动力学，且已有样本高效的策略梯度算法，但连续时间RMDP框架下的策略梯度算法尚未被充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，将策略梯度方法拓展到连续时间RMDP中。

Method: 利用路径依赖（pathwise）和伴随（adjoint-based）公式，分别从随机微分方程和常微分方程中推导策略梯度和对抗梯度。提出双循环优化器实现线性收敛，以及均场优化器作为分布优化器，并在$N$粒子近似下分析其复杂度。

Result: 双循环优化器在基于oracle的设置下达到线性收敛，在基于样本的设置下达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/ε^2)$样本复杂度；均场优化器在基于oracle的设置下达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/K)$收敛率，在$N$粒子近似下达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^2/ε)$样本复杂度。在连续时间RMDP与神经ODE动力学上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 连续时间策略梯度算法在RMDP框架中有效，两种优化器各有优势，并推导出新的分析工具适用于无折扣总成本MDP框架。

Abstract: The framework of robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) allows the design of reinforcement learning agents that satisfy performance guarantees under worst-case transition dynamics. Traditional RMDPs consider discrete-time dynamics and recently, sample-efficient policy gradient algorithms have been considered in this context. This paper investigates policy gradient algorithms within a continuous-time RMDP framework. Policy gradients and adversarial gradients are derived using pathwise and adjoint-based formulas for stochastic and ordinary differential equations. We propose double-loop optimisers to obtain linear convergence in the oracle-based setting and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{ε^2})$ sample complexity in the sample-based setting in an analysis which also derives novel tools for the framework of undiscounted total cost MDPs. Additionally, we propose mean-field optimisers as distributional optimisers with an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{K})$ oracle-based convergence rate and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{N^2}ε)$ sample complexity under $N$-particle approximation. The effectiveness of continuous-time policy gradient algorithms is confirmed for both optimisers on continuous-time RMDPs with neural ordinary differential equation dynamics.

</details>


### [180] [Federated Learning for Multi-Center Sepsis Early Prediction with Privacy-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04338)
*Xixi Tian,Di Wu,Xiang Liu,Yiziting Zhu,Yujie Li,Xin Shu,Bin Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了联邦学习在多中心脓毒症早期预测中的应用，使用来自三家中国三甲医院的648例临床样本。实验表明，联邦学习模型能达到与集中式训练相当的预测精度，同时有效避免隐私泄露，并能抵御数据重构攻击，为隐私保护下的多中心医疗协作提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心医疗数据的隐私敏感性与分布式特性严重阻碍了基于集中式建模的脓毒症早期准确预测，亟需一种既能保护数据隐私又能实现多中心协作建模的解决方案。

Method: 基于三家三甲医院648例临床筛选样本，以集中式训练范式为性能基线，构建横向联邦学习框架进行分布式协作建模，并通过隐私安全分析评估模型对数据重构攻击的抵抗能力。

Result: 联邦学习模型在预测精度上与集中式模型高度接近，同时从根本上避免了隐私泄露；隐私安全分析证实恶意攻击者无法从传输的模型参数中重构原始患者数据。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了联邦学习在临床脓毒症预测中的实用性与安全性，为隐私保护下的多中心医疗协作提供了可靠且可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-sensitive and distributed characteristics of multi-center medical data bring severe obstacles to centralized modeling for accurate early prediction of sepsis. Federated learning (FL) has attracted growing attention as a promising framework for collaborative model development, as it allows multiple institutions to jointly train predictive models without directly sharing or centralizing raw data. Nevertheless, its practical performance, robustness, and privacy-preserving benefits remain insufficiently evaluated using real-world clinical datasets. To bridge this gap, this study systematically examines the application of federated learning to multi-center sepsis prediction. The experimental dataset consists of 648 clinically screened samples collected from three tertiary hospitals in China, with rigorous inclusion and exclusion criteria. We establish a centralized training paradigm as the performance baseline, and then implement a horizontal federated learning framework for distributed collaborative modeling. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the federated learning-based model achieves highly comparable prediction accuracy to the centralized counterpart, while fundamentally avoiding privacy leakage. Further privacy security analysis verifies that malicious attackers cannot reconstruct the original patient data from the transmitted model parameters, indicating strong resistance against data reconstruction attacks. This work not only validates the practicality and security of federated learning in clinical sepsis prediction, but also provides a reliable and feasible solution for privacy-preserving multi-center medical collaboration.

</details>


### [181] [Literature-Guided Minimax Optimization of Virtual Epilepsy Neurostimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04339)
*Cathy Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种文献引导的最小化最大值（minimax）优化流程，结合PubMed文献挖掘、TVB癫痫模拟和大语言模型引导的黑箱优化，旨在设计最坏情况下表现最优的神经调控方案。实验表明，内在模型参数优化可在最坏情况下提升39.8%的性能，而临床风格的外部刺激搜索提升有限（1.7%），该研究为鲁棒性神经刺激设计提供了in silico概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型优化通常追求平均性能，但在神经调控中，这种策略可能无法保护对刺激最不耐受的患者，因此需要一种能最大化最坏情况奖励的鲁棒优化方法。

Method: 提出文献引导的最小化最大值（minimax）流水线：1）PubMed文献自动提取假设；2）TVB Epileptor仿真评估虚拟患者队列；3）大语言模型引导的黑箱优化器搜索参数；目标函数为最大化最坏情况下的奖励（模拟癫痫活动的负方差）。

Result: 内在模型参数优化将最坏情况奖励从-0.5285提升至-0.3182（改善39.8%）；外部刺激搜索仅改善1.7%，20人虚拟队列无显著群体获益（p=0.9019），但55%的响应率和颞叶亚组信号提示可能的局部效果。

Conclusion: 本研究为鲁棒性、文献感知的神经刺激设计提供了in silico概念验证，但外部刺激搜索的实际临床效果有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Computational models of epilepsy promise patient-specific treatment design, but most optimization workflows still search for parameters that perform well on average. In neuromodulation, this is a weak target: a protocol that improves the mean response can still fail in the patient whose network is least tolerant to stimulation. We present a literature-guided minimax pipeline that couples PubMed-scale hypothesis extraction, The Virtual Brain (TVB) Epileptor simulations, and large-language-model-guided black-box optimization. The optimizer proposes either intrinsic model-control parameters or clinically interpretable external-stimulation protocols; TVB evaluates each proposal across sampled virtual patients; and the objective maximizes worst-case reward, defined as the negative variance of simulated seizure activity. In the intrinsic model-control experiment, the best archived parameter set improved worst-case reward from -0.5285 to -0.3182, a 39.8% gain over baseline. The clinical-style external-stimulation search produced a much smaller worst-case improvement (1.7%), and a 20-patient virtual cohort showed no aggregate benefit (p=0.9019), despite a 55% responder rate and a positive temporal-lobe subgroup signal. The study should be read as an in silico proof of concept for robust, literature-aware neurostimulation design, not as clinical evidence.

</details>


### [182] [Expectations vs. Realities: The Cost of MSE-Optimal Forecasting Under Conditional Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04342)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在多步时间序列预测中，点级误差指标（如MSE）与边际分布真实性之间存在不可回避的权衡。当条件不确定性存在时，任何确定性预测器无法同时最小化MSE和匹配真实边际分布。实验表明，牺牲少量MSE（≤5%）可带来显著的边际真实性提升（中位数17.3%，部分超过30%）。不同预测策略（直接多输出、递归策略、样本推断）分布在该权衡前沿的不同区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多步时间序列预测使用MSE等点级误差指标评估，隐含地将条件均值视为充分目标。作者发现，在条件不确定性下，条件期望在长时域上无法代表典型实现值，导致评估误导。需要揭示这种评估方式的根本缺陷并量化其代价。

Method: 形式化定义条件不确定性间隙（conditional uncertainty gap），证明当该间隙非零时，确定性预测器无法同时最小化MSE和匹配边际分布。在受控随机动力系统和9个真实基准上，通过实证刻画accuracy-realism前沿，量化MSE-only模型选择的实际代价。

Result: 1. 确立了点准确性与边际真实性之间存在模型无关的不可回避权衡。2. 随条件不确定性增加，可达集形成显著Pareto前沿。3. 牺牲≤5%的MSE可带来中位数17.3%的边际真实性提升，部分数据集超过30%。4. 不同预测策略系统性地分布在该前沿的不同区域：直接多输出靠近精度端，递归策略和样本推断偏向真实性端。

Conclusion: MSE-based评估在长时域预测中存在结构性失效模式，应将策略和推断选择重新理解为如何在accuracy-realism权衡中进行导航。

Abstract: Multi-step time series forecasting (MSF) is commonly evaluated using point-wise error metrics such as mean squared error (MSE), implicitly treating the conditional mean as a sufficient target. We show that this can be misleading under conditional uncertainty, where the conditional expectation becomes unrepresentative of typical realized values at longer horizons. We formalize this effect through a conditional uncertainty gap and prove that whenever this gap is nonzero, no deterministic predictor can simultaneously minimize MSE and match the marginal distribution of realized futures. This establishes a fundamental, model-agnostic trade-off between point accuracy and marginal realism in MSF evaluation. Using controlled stochastic dynamical systems and nine real-world forecasting benchmarks, we empirically characterize the resulting accuracy--realism frontier and \textbf{quantify the practical cost of MSE-only model selection}. As conditional uncertainty increases with forecast horizon, the attainable set expands into a pronounced Pareto front, separating MSE-optimal but under-dispersed predictors from methods that trade accuracy for realistic marginal variability. \textbf{Across benchmarks, we find that small relaxations in MSE ($\boldsymbol{\le 5\%}$) frequently unlock disproportionate gains in marginal realism, with median improvements of $\mathbf{17.3\%}$ and gains exceeding $\mathbf{30\%}$ in some datasets.} We further show that common forecasting strategies systematically occupy different regions of this frontier: direct multi-output predictors concentrate near the accuracy-optimal extreme, while recursive strategies and sample-based inference favors marginal realism. Together, these results expose a structural failure mode of MSE-based evaluation in long-horizon forecasting and recast strategy and inference selection as navigation of an unavoidable accuracy--realism trade-off.

</details>


### [183] [MeshTok: Efficient Multi-Scale Tokenization for Scalable PDE Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04366)
*Yanshun Zhao,Xiaoyu Peng,Jiamin Jiang,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MeshTok，一种受自适应网格细化启发的令牌化与序列建模框架，通过选择性细化具有陡峭梯度、瞬态特征或多尺度结构的空间区域，生成异构多尺度令牌，在统一Transformer序列中同时捕获粗粒度全局上下文和细粒度局部细节，相比均匀网格基线持续改善效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于均匀空间划分的补丁化Transformer在处理复杂PDE解时，计算资源均匀分布，无法高效表示和求解，缺乏对局部特征的适应性。

Method: 受自适应网格细化(AMR)启发，在固定模拟网格上选择性细化关键空间区域，生成异构多尺度令牌集，并通过统一Transformer序列处理，无需专门架构组件。

Result: 在多个PDE族和基准数据集上，MeshTok持续改善效率-精度权衡，优于均匀网格基线。

Conclusion: 自适应多尺度令牌化是神经PDE建模中可扩展且可泛化的设计原则。

Abstract: Conventional patchified Transformers operate on uniform spatial partitions, distributing computational effort evenly across the domain irrespective of local features. This inflexible tokenization scheme is inherently limited in its ability to efficiently represent and process solutions to complex PDEs. To address this, we propose MeshTok, an adaptive mesh refinement (AMR)-inspired tokenization and sequence modeling framework. This method selectively refines spatial regions exhibiting sharp gradients, transient features, or multiscale structures, generating a heterogeneous set of multiscale tokens defined on a fixed simulation grid. These tokens are processed within a unified Transformer sequence, enabling the model to simultaneously capture coarse-grained global context and fine-grained local details without requiring specialized architectural components. Although adaptive refinement moderately increases token count, it promotes a more targeted allocation of computational resources to physically informative regions, which we view as a practical inductive bias rather than a formal optimality guarantee. Experimental evaluations across multiple PDE families and benchmark datasets demonstrate that MeshTok consistently improves the efficiency-accuracy trade-off compared to uniform-grid baselines. This suggests adaptive multiscale tokenization as a scalable and generalizable design principle for neural PDE modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/SCAILab-USTC/MeshTok.

</details>


### [184] [When Do Fewer Coordinates Suffice in DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04375)
*Huiqi Zhang,Fang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TP-TopK方法，通过私有的预热阶段识别坐标支撑集，将DP-SGD的噪声尺度从全参数维度d降低到活跃维度k，实验证明在活跃维度小且预热得分信息量大时效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD为每个坐标注入噪声，噪声能量随参数维度d增长。当模型具有坐标稀疏性时，能否只更新部分坐标以减少噪声而不损失优化信号？

Method: 提出TP-TopK（两阶段TopK DP-SGD）：第一阶段（私有预热）通过DP-SGD识别坐标支撑集；第二阶段（主训练）仅更新支撑集内的坐标。给出了坐标限制有益的条件判据，以及预热阶段坐标排序可靠性的下界。

Result: 非凸驻点界表明在满足条件时噪声项从O(d)降至O(k)；MNIST、FMNIST、CIFAR-10实验表明学习到的坐标支撑集比随机支撑集保留更多梯度能量，活跃维度越小、预热得分信息量越大时增益最大。

Conclusion: 在无公开数据条件下，通过私有预热阶段识别稀疏坐标支撑集可以显著降低DP-SGD的噪声维度，为大规模模型的差分隐私训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) injects noise into every updated coordinate, making the injected noise energy scale with the ambient parameter dimension \(d\). We ask when private training can update fewer coordinates without losing the signal needed for optimization. We propose \textsc{TP-TopK} (Two-Phase TopK DP-SGD), a two-phase method for coordinate-sparse private training without public data, in which a private warm-up phase identifies a coordinate support used to guide the main training phase. We give a criterion characterizing when coordinate restriction can be beneficial, show via a nonconvex stationarity bound that under this condition the relevant noise term scales with the active dimension \(k\) rather than the full parameter dimension \(d\), and provide a lower bound on the reliability of warm-up-based coordinate ranking. Experiments on MNIST, FMNIST, and CIFAR-10 show that learned coordinate supports can retain more gradient energy than size-matched random supports, with the largest gains when the active dimension is small and warm-up scores are informative.

</details>


### [185] [From Symbolic to Geometric: Enabling Spatial Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04381)
*Chen Chu,Bita Azarijoo,Li Xiong,Khurram Shafique,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**空间语言模型（SLM）**，这是首个将位置信息作为一等模态的多模态大语言模型，通过直接操作学习到的空间表示而非文本描述，实现了真正的几何空间推理。SLM在空间属性、距离、拓扑和相对位置任务上显著优于依赖符号推理的现有LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM看似具备空间推理能力，实则是基于空间语言的模式匹配（符号推理），而非真正的几何空间推理。由于LLM基于离散token操作，缺乏对连续空间表示、显式几何计算和结构化空间算子的原生支持。

Method: 1) 提出SLM架构，将位置信息作为一等模态，直接操作学习到的空间表示进行几何推理。2) 构建空间指令数据集（Spatial Instruction Dataset），对齐空间表示、原子几何操作和自然语言指令。3) 提出SpatialEval基准，涵盖属性、距离、拓扑和相对位置四类空间推理任务。

Result: SLM在SpatialEval基准的各项空间推理任务上显著优于依赖提示工程或文本抽象的现有LLM方法，验证了集成几何空间表示对鲁棒空间推理的益处。

Conclusion: 通过将几何空间表示作为一等模态集成到LLM推理过程中，能够有效弥补纯符号推理的不足，实现更鲁棒的空间推理能力。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) often appear to exhibit spatial reasoning ability; however, this capability is largely \emph{symbolic}, arising from pattern matching over spatial language rather than true \emph{geometric} reasoning over space. Because LLMs operate on discrete tokens, they lack native support for continuous spatial representations, explicit geometric computation, and structured spatial operators. To address this limitation, we introduce the \emph{Spatial Language Model (SLM)}, the first multimodal LLM that treats location information as a first-class modality and enables geometric spatial reasoning within the model's inference process. SLM directly operates on learned spatial representations rather than textual descriptions of spatial relations. To support effective training, we construct a \emph{Spatial Instruction Dataset} that aligns spatial representations, atomic geometric operations, and natural language instructions. We further propose a new benchmark named \emph{SpatialEval}, which is designed to evaluate spatial reasoning across attributes, distance, topology, and relative-position tasks. Extensive experiments show that SLM significantly outperforms existing LLM-based approaches that rely on symbolic reasoning via prompt engineering or textual abstraction, demonstrating the benefits of integrating geometric spatial representations for robust spatial reasoning.
  Our instruction dataset, evaluation benchmark, model training codes, and models' checkpoints can be found at:
  \hyperlink{https://github.com/chuchen2017/SLM}{https://github.com/chuchen2017/SLM}.

</details>


### [186] [Revisiting Privacy Amplification by Subsampling in Selective Release DPSGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04384)
*Xiaobo Huang,Fang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对DPSGD的隐私-效用权衡问题，重新审视了选择性释放机制的隐私分析，提出了DPSR-CG算法，通过严格的隐私证明和实验验证，在保证严格隐私保证的同时实现了优异的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPSGD虽然能保护训练数据隐私，但梯度裁剪和噪声注入导致严重的效用下降和收敛缓慢。DPSUR算法虽有较好效用，但其隐私核算忽略了选择性释放机制引入的采样概率变化，导致隐私保证不严谨。

Method: 重新推导选择性释放机制的隐私分析，提出基于裁剪梯度的差分隐私选择性释放算法（DPSR-CG），在多个数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、IMDB和FMNIST等多个数据集上，DPSR-CG在保持严格隐私保证的同时取得了优异的模型性能。

Conclusion: DPSR-CG通过改进的隐私分析解决了DPSUR的隐私漏洞，实现了隐私与效用的更好平衡。

Abstract: Machine learning's reliance on sensitive data necessitates privacy-preserving techniques like Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DPSGD). However, DPSGD suffers from substantial utility degradation and slow convergence due to gradient clipping and noise injection. Prior works have attempted to improve DPSGD from various perspectives; notably, the Differentially Private Selective Update and Release (DPSUR) algorithm has achieved remarkable model utility. However, the privacy accounting in DPSUR overlooks the variation in sampling probability introduced by the selective release mechanism, which compromises the rigor of its privacy guarantees. To address these limitations, we re-evaluate the privacy analysis of the selective release mechanism and propose a novel algorithm: Differentially Private Selective Release based on Clipped Gradients (DPSR-CG). Through a rigorous, newly derived privacy analysis and extensive experiments on multiple datasets (MNIST, CIFAR-10, IMDB, and FMNIST), we demonstrate that our DPSR-CG mechanism maintains strict privacy guarantees while achieving exceptional model performance.

</details>


### [187] [Shortcomings and capacities of real-constrained neural networks in complex spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04390)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在复杂假设类中强制实预激活与复预激活之间的存储容量渐近比，利用Gardner体积比较和HCIZ公式在临界容量下进行推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究实预激活与复预激活条件下存储容量的渐近比例关系，以理解不同激活约束对假设类容量的影响。

Method: 采用Gardner体积比较方法在临界容量处进行分析，并借助Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber（HCIZ）公式（该公式在该文献中非常规应用）进行推导。通过Weyl积分公式和Haar测度在酉流形和正交紧流形上积分，获得更稳健的渐近比近似。

Result: 得到了实预激活与复预激活条件下存储容量的渐近比率。

Conclusion: HCIZ公式的应用使得在紧流形上的积分处理更加稳健，该策略有效解决了特定假设类下的容量比较问题。

Abstract: We find the asymptotic ratio between the storage capacities when enforcing real pre-activations in a complex hypothesis class as opposed to complex ones in the same class. Our methods depend on Gardner volume comparisons at critical capacity. Our proof relies on an application of the Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber (HCIZ) formula, nonstandard in literature. With the HCIZ formula, we may obtain a more robust approximation for the final asymptotic ratio. This strategy is applicable to our work specifically since we integrate over the unitary and orthogonal compact manifolds, facilitated via the Weyl integration formula and the Haar measure.

</details>


### [188] [Physics-Informed Neural Network Modeling of Biodegradable Contaminant Transport through GCL/SL Composite Liners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04392)
*Dong Li,Yapeng Cao,Haiping Zhao,Shutong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种双域物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于模拟GCL/SL复合衬垫系统中的污染物运移。其中，GCL薄层采用稳态平流-弥散-生物降解模型，而土质衬垫则视为瞬态运移域。对比了标准软约束PINN（Std-PINN）和硬约束PINN（H-PINN）的性能，后者将边界和初始条件直接嵌入试解中。H-PINN显著提高了预测精度（MAE从0.058-0.067降至0.011-0.023，MRE从9.10%-19.16%降至2.08%-3.14%），并成功应用于反问题建模以识别半衰期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有污染物运移数值模拟方法在处理复合衬垫系统（GCL/SL）时面临薄层与厚层多尺度耦合的挑战，传统方法计算成本高且精度有限。本研究旨在开发一种高效、准确的PINN框架，实现对GCL/SL复合衬垫系统中污染物运移的正向模拟和逆向参数识别。

Method: 提出双域PINN框架：GCL层采用稳态平流-弥散-生物降解方程，SL层采用瞬态运移方程。对比两种形式：Std-PINN（软约束，通过损失函数惩罚项施加边界条件）和H-PINN（硬约束，将边界/初始条件直接嵌入网络输出解）。采用tanh激活函数并优化网络结构，进行正向模拟和反问题参数识别。

Result: Std-PINN能捕捉整体穿透行为，但早期阶段误差较大，尤其在高水头（平流主导）条件下。H-PINN将MAE从0.058-0.067降至0.011-0.023，MRE从9.10%-19.16%降至2.08%-3.14%，预测更准确稳定。在反问题中，H-PINN能从有限浓度观测中可靠收敛到预设的SL降解半衰期值，且在低至中等观测噪声下具有可接受的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 硬约束PINN（H-PINN）是处理GCL/SL复合衬垫系统污染物运移问题的有效方法，在正向模拟精度和反问题参数识别可靠性方面均优于标准软约束PINN。tanh激活函数配合优化的网络结构可获得最佳预测性能。
