Deep Koopman methods learn flexible coordinates, whereas structure-preserving methods enforce operator identities on fixed dictionaries. We combine these ideas by introducing Deep Embedded Multiplicative Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DeepMDMD), a method that learns a latent space and a partition of it, while enforcing the Koopman product rule as an exact algebraic constraint. Training alternates between an exact multiplicative operator update and a differentiable latent-clustering step that promotes Koopman closure. The result is a finite transition map on learned latent cells. Its nonzero spectrum lies on the unit circle, its dictionary is shaped by the dynamics rather than by ambient geometry, and forecasts are made in latent coordinates before being decoded to physical space. Across Hamiltonian, chaotic, and fluid examples, DeepMDMD learns dictionaries that are far more compact and dynamically coherent than those produced by geometric MDMD partitions. It reduces spectral pollution, reveals richer continuous-spectrum structure, and gives stable forecasts under severe noise. In high-dimensional flows, including a 158,624-dimensional cylinder wake and a noisy $Re=20,000$ lid-driven cavity, it preserves coherent structures and long-time spectral statistics where state-space MDMD fails. These results suggest a practical rule for Koopman learning: learn the coordinates, constrain the algebra.

</details>


### [264] [Generating Financial Time Series by Matching Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05138)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Nikita Zozoulenko,Ben Wood,Thomas Cass,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOCK（SOft Competing Kernels），一种完全可微的随机卷积特征映射，用于解决金融时间序列生成中因数据稀缺导致的过拟合问题。通过匹配真实与生成时间序列的随机卷积特征来训练生成器，SOCK在少量金融数据集上持续优于签名和扩散基线方法，并在双样本假设检验和时间序列分类任务中达到或超越现有无监督特征映射的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列的生成面临训练数据稀缺（仅单个历史路径）的挑战，这使得对抗训练中判别器容易记忆训练样本而导致过拟合。现有方法通过匹配路径签名等未训练特征表示来缓解此问题，但路径签名在可处理的截断深度下无法捕获相关的时间序列属性。

Method: 提出SOCK（SOft Competing Kernels），一种完全可微的随机卷积特征映射。与现有不可微的随机卷积特征映射（如Rocket、Hydra）不同，SOCK通过设计软竞争机制实现可微性，从而能够用于监督生成式时间序列模型的训练。具体地，通过匹配真实与生成时间序列的随机SOCK特征来训练生成器。

Result: 在多种小样本金融数据集上，基于SOCK特征匹配训练的生成器一致优于签名（signature）和扩散（diffusion）基线方法。在双样本假设检验和时间序列分类任务中，SOCK匹配或超越了现有无监督特征映射的性能。

Conclusion: SOCK作为可微的随机卷积特征映射，有效解决了金融时间序列生成中的数据稀缺和过拟合问题，并在多个任务上展现了优越的表达能力。

Abstract: Generating realistic financial time series is challenging as training data is often limited to a single historical path. With such scarce data, overfitting is hard to avoid, especially under adversarial training where a trained discriminator can memorize the training samples. To mitigate this, recent approaches train generators to minimize the discrepancy between untrained feature representations of real and generated time series. In these works, the feature maps are based on path signatures, which can fail to capture relevant time series properties at tractable truncation depths. In this work, we instead train generators by matching random convolutional features of real and generated time series. Existing random convolutional feature maps, such as Rocket and Hydra, have been shown to provide informative representations of real-world time series, but cannot supervise generative models because they are non-differentiable. We introduce SOCK (SOft Competing Kernels), a fully differentiable random convolutional feature map, suited to train generative time series models. We show that generators trained by matching random SOCK features consistently outperform signature and diffusion baselines across a wide range of small-sample financial datasets. We further demonstrate SOCK's expressiveness on two-sample hypothesis testing and time series classification tasks, where SOCK matches or outperforms existing unsupervised feature maps.

</details>


### [265] [BBOmix: A Tabular Benchmark for Hyperparameter Optimization of Unsupervised Biological Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05139)
*Luca Thale-Bombien,Jan Ewald,Ralf König,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BBOmix，首个针对生物数据无监督表示学习的开源表格基准，包含105,000次评估，涵盖四种自编码器架构和七种多组学模态，并系统评估了多种超参数优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多组学数据中，自编码器虽被广泛用于降维和表示学习，但其对架构和超参数高度敏感，且无监督优化依赖的重构损失可能与下游任务效用相关性差。穷举超参数优化计算成本高昂，导致研究者常使用次优默认配置。为降低大规模无监督超参数优化研究的门槛，亟需一个标准化基准。

Method: 构建了BBOmix基准，包含105,000次实验评估，覆盖四种AE架构（如标准AE、变分AE等）在TCGA和SCHC数据集上的七种多组学模态。量化了重构损失与下游任务性能的相关性，并评估了单保真度、多保真度和迁移学习三类HPO方法。

Result: 建立了无监督生物表示学习的严格基线，提供了重构损失与下游任务性能相关性的量化分析，并比较了各类HPO方法在基准上的表现。

Conclusion: BBOmix填补了生物无监督表示学习领域标准化基准的空白，为未来研究提供了可复现的评估平台和基线结果，有助于推动该领域HPO方法的开发与比较。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high-throughput sequencing has led to large, high-dimensional omics datasets. Deep unsupervised learning architectures, particularly Autoencoders (AEs), are increasingly used for dimensionality reduction and representation learning in this domain. However, AEs are highly sensitive to architectural choices and hyperparameters, and unsupervised optimization typically relies on reconstruction loss, which may be a poor proxy for downstream utility. Exhaustive hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is computationally expensive, leading researchers to frequently rely on suboptimal default configurations. To democratize access to large-scale unsupervised HPO research, we introduce $\textbf{BBOmix}$, the first open-source tabular benchmark for unsupervised representation learning on real-world biological data. Our benchmark includes 105,000 evaluations across four AE architectures and seven multi-omics modalities from the TCGA and SCHC datasets. We quantify the correlation between reconstruction loss and downstream task performance and provide an extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art single-fidelity, multi-fidelity, and transfer learning HPO methods, establishing a rigorous baseline for future research in unsupervised biological representation learning.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Semantic Constraint Synthesis for Adaptive Trajectory Optimization via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04123)
*Eleanor Brosius,Yuji Takubo,Daniele Gammelli,Simone D'Amico,Marco Pavone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用大语言模型(LLMs)将自然语言描述的任务需求自动转化为可执行的轨迹优化代码及数学公式的框架，在航天器交会场景中验证了该方法能够高成功率地从语义需求中重构凸轨迹优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着太空任务频率和复杂度的增加，需要快速构建数学上严谨的轨迹优化问题，但将任务意图转化为可处理的分析公式需要大量领域专业知识，降低了任务设计效率。

Method: 提出一个基于大语言模型(LLMs)的框架，将自然语言描述的任务需求和约束自动翻译为可执行的轨迹优化代码及对应的数学公式表达。

Result: 在航天器交会场景的实验中，该方法能够从语义任务需求中高成功率地重构凸轨迹优化问题。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了LLMs弥合高层意图与形式化优化模型之间鸿沟的潜力，能够实现更加灵活高效的航天器轨迹设计。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization is a critical component for enabling safe and reliable autonomous operations in space exploration. As space missions increase in frequency, complexity, and scope, there is a growing need to rapidly formulate mathematically sound trajectory optimization problems that accurately reflect mission objectives and operational constraints. However, translating mission intent into tractable analytical formulations for trajectory optimization requires substantial domain expertise. This paper presents a framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to translate natural language descriptions of mission requirements and constraints into executable trajectory optimization code and corresponding mathematical formulations. Experiments in spacecraft rendezvous scenarios demonstrate a high success rate in reconditioning a convex trajectory optimization problem from semantic mission requirements. Ultimately, this work highlights the potential of LLMs to bridge high-level intent and formal optimization models, enabling more flexible and efficient trajectory design of spacecraft.

</details>


### [267] [A duality approach to the dense graph limit for biological transportation networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04129)
*Nuno J. Alves,Jan Haskovec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对生物运输网络的变分模型，提出了基于对偶形式的稠密图极限理论。与早期需要电导率一致正的压力方法不同，新方法允许一般非负电导率核，通过对偶变分原理定义动能，并证明了半离散网络能量在Mosco意义下Γ-收敛到连续能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服先前基于压力方法要求电导率一致为正的限制，使公式能够处理一般的非负电导率核，从而更广泛地适用于生物运输网络建模。

Method: 采用对偶变分原理定义动能，该原理对退化可积核仍然有意义，并在关联非局部泊松问题不可解时赋予无限能量；通过正则化电导率核构造Γ-limsup恢复序列，利用对偶公式直接推导Γ-liminf不等式。

Result: 在由代谢项决定的自然L^γ拓扑下，证明了半离散网络能量在Mosco意义下Γ-收敛到连续能量，其中Γ-liminf不等式直接来自对偶公式，Γ-limsup恢复序列通过电导率核的正则化构造。

Conclusion: 建立了一种更通用的对偶公式化稠密图极限方法，允许非负电导率核，为生物运输网络的连续极限分析提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a duality-based formulation of the dense graph limit for a variational model of biological transportation networks, where edge conductivities balance pumping power against metabolic cost. In contrast to the pressure-based approach of our previous work, which required conductivities to be uniformly positive, the present formulation allows general nonnegative conductivity kernels. The kinetic energy is defined through a dual variational principle, which remains meaningful for degenerate integrable kernels and assigns infinite energy when the associated nonlocal Poisson problem is not solvable. Using this formulation, we prove $Γ$-convergence in the sense of Mosco of the semidiscrete network energies to a continuum energy on symmetric nonnegative kernels. The convergence is obtained in the natural $L^γ$ topology dictated by the metabolic term. The $Γ$-$\liminf$ inequality follows directly from the dual formulation, while $Γ$-$\limsup$ recovery sequences are constructed by positive regularization of the conductivity kernels.

</details>


### [268] [Nonlocal Mean Field Schrödinger Bridge with Learned Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04265)
*Daisuke Inoue,Mathieu Laurière,Dante Kalise*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出用神经网络代理近似非局部相互作用，将均场薛定谔桥问题的计算复杂度从二次降至线性，并推导了代理误差传播的稳定性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均场薛定谔桥问题中非局部相互作用项的计算复杂度随群体规模呈二次增长，使得大规模问题难以求解。

Method: 采用神经网络代理近似非局部相互作用项，设计四阶段交替算法，将推理时每步成本从二次降至线性。

Result: 在导航和意见动力学任务上，该方法能复现解析评估得到的轨迹，并减少训练时间。

Conclusion: 神经网络代理近似有效解决了均场薛定谔桥问题的计算瓶颈，在保持精度的同时大幅提升效率。

Abstract: The Schrödinger Bridge Problem constructs a stochastic process that connects an initial distribution to a terminal distribution with minimum energy. This work considers its mean-field extension, the Mean-Field Schrödinger Bridge, for interacting particle systems. With nonlocal interactions, evaluating the resulting particle-dependent distributional terms can scale quadratically with the population size, which makes large-scale problems intractable. We address this bottleneck by approximating the nonlocal interactions with neural network surrogates. The resulting four-stage alternating algorithm reduces the per-step cost from quadratic to linear in the population size at inference. We also derive Grönwall-type stability bounds that show how surrogate errors propagate to the generated trajectories. In numerical experiments on navigation and opinion-dynamics tasks, the proposed method reproduces trajectories obtained with analytical evaluation and reduces training time.

</details>


### [269] [A Perturbed q-Tsallis Self-Concordant Barrier for Spectrally Robust Semidefinite Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04348)
*Sergio Assuncao Monteiro,Fabricio Alves Barbosa da Silva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了半定规划（SDP）的一种扰动q-Tsallis障碍函数，通过谱扰动经典log-det障碍函数引入特征值自适应硬化。理论贡献包括：精确刻画了该障碍函数的微分自和谐性，证明当且仅当q∈(1,2]时对所有η≥0微分自和谐成立；在紧致可行集上保持O(n)的局部障碍参数；中心路径对扰动的敏感性在小特征值方向按κ(X^*)^(-(q-1))尺度选择性衰减，提高了病态SDP解的鲁棒性。此外，开发了配备Lanczos Krylov核的Mehrotra型原-对偶预估-校正内点法，数值实验验证了理论预测并展示了显著的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典log-det障碍函数在处理病态半定规划问题时，小特征值方向的灵敏度高，导致收敛缓慢或数值不稳定。本文旨在通过引入Tsallis型矩阵幂项构造一种新的障碍函数，实现特征值自适应刚度调节，从而在保持理论收敛性的同时提高对病态问题的鲁棒性和计算效率。

Method: (1) 提出扰动q-Tsallis障碍函数：ψ(X) = -log det X + η·(1/(q-1))·Tr(X^{q-1})，其中q>1, η≥0；(2) 理论分析微分自和谐性条件，证明全局自和谐当且仅当q∈(1,2]；(3) 建立谱鲁棒性分析，刻画中心路径敏感性在特征值方向的阻尼特性；(4) 设计Mehrotra型原-对偶预估-校正内点法，采用Lanczos Krylov子空间方法高效计算矩阵幂。

Result: (1) 严格证明了全局微分自和谐的阈值：q∈(1,2]对所有η≥0成立，q>2时失败；(2) 紧致可行集上局部障碍参数为O(n)，保持经典log-det的迭代复杂度；(3) 小特征值方向的灵敏度按κ(X^*)^(-(q-1))尺度衰减，q>1时优于经典log-det；(4) 数值实验表明，该方法在病态SDP上具有更好的鲁棒性和显著的计算加速效果。

Conclusion: 扰动q-Tsallis障碍函数通过谱自适应硬化机制，在理论自和谐性与实际计算鲁棒性之间取得了更好的平衡，尤其适合处理病态半定规划问题。结合高效的Krylov子空间矩阵幂计算，该方法在保持最优迭代复杂度的同时显著改善了数值稳定性和求解效率。

Abstract: We introduce and analyse a perturbed $q$-Tsallis barrier for semidefinite programming (SDP), defined as a spectral perturbation of the classical log-det barrier on the cone of positive definite matrices. The barrier introduces eigenvalue-adaptive stiffening through a Tsallis-type matrix-power term controlled by parameters $q>1$ and $η\geq0$. Our main theoretical contribution is a sharp characterisation of the differential self-concordance regime of the barrier. We prove that the barrier is differentially self-concordant on the interior of the positive semidefinite cone for all $η\geq0$ if and only if $q\in(1,2]$, establishing the exact threshold at $q=2$. For $q>2$, uniform self-concordance fails globally, although local sufficient conditions remain valid on compact spectral domains. On compact feasible sets, the effective local barrier parameter remains $O(n)$, preserving the same asymptotic iteration complexity class as the classical log-det barrier. We further establish a spectral robustness result showing that the sensitivity of the central path to perturbations is selectively damped in small-eigenvalue directions according to the scaling $κ(X^*)^{-(q-1)}$, where $κ(X^*)$ denotes the spectral condition number. This yields improved robustness relative to the classical log-det barrier for ill-conditioned SDP solutions. Finally, we develop a Mehrotra-type primal--dual predictor--corrector interior-point method equipped with a Lanczos-based Krylov kernel for evaluating matrix powers efficiently. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical predictions and demonstrate improved robustness together with significant computational acceleration.

</details>


### [270] [Optimal Control of Sweeping Processes with Nonsmooth Moving Sets: A Numerical Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04639)
*Chadi Nour*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将非光滑 sweeping 过程数值方法从自治情形推广到非自治情形，其中 sweeping 集显式依赖于时间。通过建立移动约束集及其边界的均匀几何估计，克服了时间依赖带来的困难，证明了离散逼近格式的收敛性，并给出数值算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有针对自治情形的非光滑 sweeping 过程数值方法推广到更一般且更具挑战性的非自治情形（sweeping 集显式依赖于时间），以处理移动约束集带来的额外困难。

Method: 建立移动约束集及其边界的均匀几何估计，以此克服时间依赖导致几何常数先验依赖于时间的问题，进而扩展了de Pinho等人[22]以及Nour和Zeidan[33, 37]提出的离散逼近格式。

Result: 证明了所提出的离散逼近格式收敛到原问题的容许解，并通过数值算例展示了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 成功将非光滑 sweeping 过程的数值方法从自治情形推广至非自治情形，均匀几何估计是处理时间依赖约束集的关键技术。

Abstract: This paper extends the numerical method introduced by de Pinho et al. [22] and later developed in Nour and Zeidan [33, 37] to the nonautonomous case where the nonsmooth sweeping set depends explicitly on time. The moving character of the sweeping set creates substantial additional difficulties, since several geometric constants involved in the analysis may a priori depend on time. To overcome this issue, we establish uniform geometric estimates for the moving constraint sets and their boundaries. These estimates allow us to extend the discrete approximation scheme developed in [22, 33, 37] and prove its convergence toward admissible solutions of the original problem. Several examples illustrating the applicability of the proposed method are also presented.

</details>


### [271] [Near-Optimal Decentralized Stochastic Convex Optimization over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04757)
*Nitai Kluger,Amit Attia,Tomer Koren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究去中心化随机平滑凸优化问题，提出一种加速去中心化方法，在总梯度样本预算N下，可将保持集中式O(1/√N)统计速率的最大worker数量从M≲ρ√N提升至M≲√ρ N^{3/4}（ρ为 gossip 网络谱间隙），并证明了该方法的线性跨度最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化随机优化中，一个核心问题是在总梯度样本预算N固定的条件下，最多能使用多少个worker仍然保持集中式优化的O(1/√N)统计速率。此前最优结果为M≲ρ√N，本文试图突破这一上界。

Method: 提出一种基于一步延迟的随机加速方案（one-step-delayed stochastic acceleration），使workers能够将minibatching与加速gossip交错进行，同时控制残余不一致性。该方法对局部最优异质性的依赖仅为对数形式。

Result: 将该设置下保持集中式统计速率的最大worker数量从M≲ρ√N提升至M≲√ρ N^{3/4}。同时建立了线性跨度去中心化一阶方法的匹配下界，证明该方法在log因子意义下最优。

Conclusion: 本文提出的加速去中心化方法在统计效率与可扩展性之间取得了更优的权衡，且达到了线性跨度去中心化一阶方法的最优性能。

Abstract: We study decentralized stochastic smooth convex optimization, where $M$ workers minimize an average objective using local stochastic gradients and neighbor-only communication over a fixed gossip network. A central question in this setting is to determine the largest number of workers that can be used under a total budget of $N$ gradient samples while still preserving the centralized $O(1/\sqrt N)$ statistical rate. We introduce an accelerated decentralized method that preserves this rate for up to $\smash{M\lesssim \sqrtρ\,N^{3/4}}$ workers, where $ρ$ is the spectral gap of the gossip network, improving the best prior maximal scaling of $\smash{M\lesssim ρ\sqrt N}$. The method is based on a one-step-delayed stochastic acceleration scheme that enables workers to interleave minibatching with accelerated gossip while controlling residual disagreement, and its guarantee depends only logarithmically on the optimum-local heterogeneity. We also establish a matching lower bound for linear-span decentralized first-order methods, showing that the method is optimal up to logarithmic factors.

</details>
